# M1 P0 Issue Queue

This queue turns the six-month roadmap and Hermes-style planning supplement into assignable Month 1 work. Each issue must use `docs/issues/NBG_MULTI_AGENT_ISSUE_TEMPLATE.md` before implementation.

## Queue Rules

- Every issue has an owner Agent and a review Agent.
- Security-sensitive work requires Security Agent review.
- UI changes require screenshots for light, black, Claude themes when relevant.
- Runtime changes require logs for success, failure, and recovery.
- Merge evidence must include commands run and known follow-ups.

## Issue 1: Engineering Planning System

Owner Agent: Coordinator Agent

Review Agent: Review Agent

Security Review: not-required

Goal:

- Establish the durable planning system requested in the Hermes-style supplement.

Files/Areas:

- `AGENTS.md`
- `docs/plans/NBG_PLAN_TEMPLATE.md`

- `docs/issues/NBG_MULTI_AGENT_ISSUE_TEMPLATE.md`

Acceptance:

- A new Agent can pick up a task with goal, scope, files, tests, risks, owner, reviewer, and merge evidence.
- Plans use Summary, Problem Frame, Requirements, Decisions, Design, Units, Boundaries, Risks, Verification, Sources.
- Contracts include participants, trust boundary, data structures, state machine, endpoint/event semantics, errors, compatibility, security rules, and test oracle.

Verification:

- Inspect all files listed above.
- Use this queue to create at least three concrete follow-up issues.

## Issue 2: Disable App Data Backup For Local Secrets

Status: code complete, verified by unit test and debug APK build.

Owner Agent: Security Agent

Review Agent: Android Agent

Security Review: required

Goal:

- Prevent Android cloud/device backup from copying SharedPreferences that may contain URL API keys, FTP passwords, Memory, and local service config.

Files/Areas:

- `app/src/main/AndroidManifest.xml`
- `app/src/test/java/com/nbg/android/AndroidManifestBehaviorTest.kt`

Acceptance:

- `android:allowBackup` is `false`.
- `android:fullBackupContent` is `false`.
- JVM test prevents accidental re-enable.

Verification:

```bash
./gradlew :app:testDebugUnitTest --tests com.nbg.android.AndroidManifestBehaviorTest.manifestDisablesAppDataBackupForLocalSecrets
./gradlew :app:assembleDebug
```

Evidence:

- `app/src/main/AndroidManifest.xml` sets `android:allowBackup="false"` and `android:fullBackupContent="false"`.
- `AndroidManifestBehaviorTest.manifestDisablesAppDataBackupForLocalSecrets` passes.
- `:app:assembleDebug` passes.

## Issue 3: URL API Keystore Migration

Status: code complete for URL API metadata/key separation and AndroidKeyStore-backed encrypted secret storage. Device manual save/load remains before public beta release.

Owner Agent: Security Agent

Review Agent: Android Agent

Security Review: required

Goal:

- Replace plaintext URL API `apiKey` storage in `NbgApiStore.kt` with Android Keystore backed encrypted storage.

Files/Areas:

- `app/src/main/java/com/nbg/android/NbgApiStore.kt`
- URL API UI and tests.

Acceptance:

- Existing plaintext entries load and migrate.
- New saves do not store plaintext key values in `KEY_ENTRIES`.
- Legacy plaintext is removed only after encrypted write succeeds.
- Migration failure preserves user access and surfaces a recoverable error.
- Delete removes encrypted key material for that entry.

Verification:

- Unit tests for empty key, plaintext migration, encrypted load, corrupted value, delete, and failed migration fallback.
- Manual URL API save/load on device.

Evidence:

- `NbgApiStore.kt` writes URL API metadata without `apiKey`.
- Legacy `apiKey` values migrate into `NbgEncryptedPreferenceSecretStore` through `NbgAndroidApiKeySecretStore` using AndroidKeyStore AES/GCM.
- If secret migration fails, the old plaintext metadata is not overwritten, preventing key loss.
- `NbgApiStoreTest` passes.
- `AndroidManifestBehaviorTest.urlApiPageStoresRuntimeKeysAndVerifiesUpstreamModels` passes.
- `:app:assembleDebug` passes.

## Issue 4: FTP And Local Service Trust Boundary

Status: code/contract complete for current beta boundary. FTP control/data sockets are loopback-bound and password-protected; FTP password now migrates from legacy SharedPreferences into AndroidKeyStore-backed encrypted storage. Hanako loopback health uses bearer token; Android MCP beta is private-service, loopback-bound, read-only/echo-only. Remaining work before public beta: device/network manual check and Security Agent sign-off.

Owner Agent: Security Agent

Review Agent: Runtime Reliability Agent

Security Review: required

Goal:

- Make FTP, Hanako loopback HTTP, and Android MCP beta boundaries explicit before public beta.

Files/Areas:

- `NbgFtpFileServer.kt`
- `NbgFileShareModel.kt`
- `HanakoApiClient.kt`
- `HanakoServerLauncher.kt`
- `app/src/main/res/xml/network_security_config.xml`
- `android-mcp-server/src/main/java/com/nbg/android/mcpserver`

Acceptance:

- FTP write/delete behavior has authentication and user-visible state.
- Loopback cleartext remains limited to `127.0.0.1` and `localhost`.
- Android MCP beta server has documented binding, exported state, auth/trust decision, and known beta limitations.
- Any non-loopback exposure is blocked or requires a separate plan.

Verification:

- Source tests where possible.
- Device/network manual check.
- Security review notes.

Evidence:

- `NbgFtpFileServer` binds to `127.0.0.1` through the registry path.
- `NbgFileShareModel.kt` stores FTP password through `NbgEncryptedPreferenceSecretStore`.
- Legacy FTP password is removed from `nbg_file_share_server` after encrypted migration succeeds.
- `local-service-boundary-contract.md` defines Hanako, FTP, MCP beta, cleartext, and future side-effect tool rules.
- `AndroidManifestBehaviorTest.fileSharePasswordUsesEncryptedSecretStore` passes.
- `AndroidManifestBehaviorTest.appLocalServicesStayLoopbackOnlyWithTokenOrPasswordBoundary` passes.
- `NbgFtpFileServerTest` passes.
- `McpServerBoundaryTest` passes.

- `NbgChatPreferenceStore.kt`, `HanakoBridge.kt`, and `HanakoApiClient.kt` now use `ask` as fallback default.
- `HanakoApiClient.kt` and `HanakoServerLauncher.kt` set `learn_skills.safety_review=true`.
- `NbgAgentChatUi.kt` shows risk label, target, and recovery hint in confirmation cards.

## Issue 6: Runtime Patch Version Gate

Status: code/contract complete for marker-pinned bundled runtime patching and automated diagnostics export linkage. Runtime patch scripts now record applied/skipped/failed state, skip bundled file mutation on marker mismatch, use a patch-set active version to force stale server cleanup, and diagnostics export now surfaces patch-set match, marker match state, patch counts, attention state, and a UI summary without raw marker or target path export. Remaining work before public beta: device startup log review and Security Agent sign-off with a real exported diagnostics sample.

Owner Agent: Terminal/Runtime Agent

Review Agent: Android Agent

Security Review: required

Goal:

- Make Hanako runtime patching version-gated, diagnosable, and recoverable.

Files/Areas:

- `HanakoServerLauncher.kt`
- `app/src/main/assets/hanako-server-linux-arm64-node22.nbgpack.marker`

Acceptance:

- Patch IDs record target marker/version, applied/skipped/failed state, and error.
- Marker mismatch does not blindly patch.
- Optional patch failure does not block base startup when safe.
- Diagnostics can report patch status.

Verification:

- Simulate marker match/mismatch, target missing, already applied, and patch failure.
- Launcher logs show status.

Evidence:

- `runtime-patch-gate-contract.md` defines marker, patch status, state machine, and test oracle.
- `hanako-server-linux-arm64-node22.nbgpack.marker` pins the bundled pack as `size=<bytes>;sha256=<hex>`.
- `HanakoServerLauncher.kt` reads the asset marker, records runtime patch status to `$HANA_HOME/android-runtime-patches.json`, and uses `android-runtime-patches.active` for patch-set restart gating.
- Bundled runtime file patches record `skipped` with `target-marker-mismatch` instead of mutating an unknown target.
- `NbgDiagnosticsExport.kt` reads `targetMarker` and `activeMarker` from the local runtime patch status file only to compute `targetMarkerPresent`, `activeMarkerPresent`, `markerMatched`, and `markerMatchState`; raw marker values and raw patch target paths are not exported.
- `NbgDiagnosticsExportUi.kt` shows a diagnostics dialog status line derived from the exported redacted JSON, including runtime patch state, active/set versions, marker match label, patch counts, and attention count.
- `NbgDiagnosticsExportTest.runtimePatchReaderExposesMarkerGateWithoutRawMarkersOrTargets` verifies marker mismatch diagnostics using temporary runtime patch files and asserts raw marker values, target paths, and token-like errors are absent from the export.
- Targeted verification passes: `./gradlew --no-daemon :app:testDebugUnitTest --tests com.nbg.android.AndroidManifestBehaviorTest.androidHanakoServerUsesStableUpstreamUserAgent --tests com.nbg.android.AndroidManifestBehaviorTest.bundledHanakoServerPackOnlyShipsNbgEngineeringCoreSkill`.
- Full JVM verification passes: `./gradlew --no-daemon :app:testDebugUnitTest :terminal-core:testDebugUnitTest :android-mcp-server:testDebugUnitTest`.
- Debug APK build passes: `./gradlew --no-daemon :app:assembleDebug :android-mcp-server:assembleDebug`.
- APK asset check passes: `unzip -p app/build/outputs/apk/debug/app-debug.apk assets/hanako-server-linux-arm64-node22.nbgpack.marker`.

- `NbgAgentToolStatusState.kt` owns pending tool status buffering, stable-key normalization, merge-before-flush behavior, immediate flush decisions for terminal/file completion, and drain/clear lifecycle.
- `NbgAgentShellState.kt`, `NbgAgentFlushSchedulerState.kt`, `NbgAgentUrlApiEntriesState.kt`, `NbgAgentUrlApiSelectionState.kt`, `NbgAgentChatPreferenceState.kt`, `NbgAgentUserMessageDedupState.kt`, `NbgAgentStreamingControllerState.kt`, `NbgAgentConfirmationState.kt`, `NbgAgentDiagnosticsExportState.kt`, `NbgAgentFileShareState.kt`, `NbgAgentModelConfigState.kt`, `NbgAgentTodoState.kt`, `NbgAgentContentBlockPatchState.kt`, `NbgAgentMessageState.kt`, `NbgAgentHistoryState.kt`, `NbgAgentStreamingTextState.kt`, `NbgAgentSkillTranslationState.kt`, and `NbgAgentPetState.kt` continue the incremental state extraction for shell page state, flush scheduler job state, URL API entries loaded/list state, URL API selected model/default restore/chat preference restore state, chat preference loaded/current state, user-message dedup state, streaming controller state, confirmation/revert dialog state, diagnostics export dialog state, file share dialog/service state, model config load/error state, session todo state, content block patch buffering, chat message indexes, deferred history/session selection, streaming text buffers, Skill translation UI state, and PetDex/Pet UI state.

- `NbgAgentUi.kt` now creates `val flushSchedulerState = remember { NbgAgentFlushSchedulerState() }`, delegates streaming/tool flush Job ownership to the holder, and keeps delay durations, flush contents, deferred-history checks, and coroutine launch ownership in UI orchestration.
- `NbgAgentUi.kt` now creates `val urlApiEntriesState = remember { NbgAgentUrlApiEntriesState() }`, reads `savedApis` and `savedApisLoaded` from the holder, and keeps `NbgApiStore` encrypted persistence plus Hanako provider sync in UI orchestration.
- `NbgAgentUi.kt` now creates `val urlApiSelectionState = remember { NbgAgentUrlApiSelectionState() }`, reads selected URL API model and restore flags from the holder, and keeps URL API validation, preference persistence, Hanako model switching, provider sync, send, and new-session URL API resolution in UI orchestration.
- `NbgAgentUi.kt` now creates `val chatPreferenceState = remember { NbgAgentChatPreferenceState() }`, reads `chatPreferences` and `chatPreferencesLoaded` from the holder, and keeps `NbgChatPreferenceStore` persistence plus appearance callbacks in UI orchestration.
- `NbgAgentUi.kt` now creates `val userMessageDedupState = remember { NbgAgentUserMessageDedupState() }`, keeps display-text normalization and message append in UI, and delegates the 1.5 second duplicate-prompt guard to a unit-tested holder.
- `NbgAgentUi.kt` now creates `val streamingControllerState = remember { NbgAgentStreamingControllerState() }`, keeps visible chat streaming tied to `hanakoState.streaming`, and delegates the live-work controller flag used by deferred history gating to a unit-tested holder.
- `NbgAgentUi.kt` now creates `val toolStatusState = remember { NbgAgentToolStatusState() }` and keeps coroutine flush scheduling in UI while delegating queue semantics to a unit-tested holder.
- `NbgAgentUi.kt` now creates `val contentBlockPatchState = remember { NbgAgentContentBlockPatchState() }` and keeps existing content block message mutation while delegating patch queue, merge, consume, and clear semantics to a unit-tested holder.
- Stale fetch/verify guards still check current request serial, URL snapshot, API key snapshot, and model existence before applying results.
- `NbgAgentApiEditorStateTest` covers open/close, credential change reset, request serial checks, fetch/verify result application, and verified-only save selection.

## Issue 13: Skill Source Integrity Contract And Provenance

Status: code/contract/test complete for source review, provenance, diagnostics redaction, and Android-pinned bundled Skill default-enable gating. Default bundled Skills now require Android source-pinned size/SHA-256 checks, server-pack `skills2set` byte equivalence, trusted bundled source classification, and same-name untrusted Skill removal before automatic enablement. Verification remains debug-only and does not run release/R8.

Owner Agent: Security Agent

Review Agent: QA/Release Agent

Security Review: required

Goal:

- Prevent untrusted external, URL, user-managed, or Agent-written Skills from silently becoming trusted defaults, and make manual Skill installs reviewable without leaking source paths or URLs.

Files/Areas:

- `docs/contracts/skill-source-integrity-contract.md`
- `docs/contracts/README.md`
- `app/src/main/java/com/nbg/android/NbgSkillSourceIntegrity.kt`
- `app/src/main/java/com/nbg/android/HanakoServerLauncher.kt`
- `app/src/main/java/com/nbg/android/HanakoApiClient.kt`
- `app/src/main/java/com/nbg/android/HanakoChatController.kt`
- `app/src/main/java/com/nbg/android/HanakoSkillInstallSupport.kt`
- `app/src/main/java/com/nbg/android/NbgDiagnosticsExport.kt`
- `app/src/test/java/com/nbg/android/NbgSkillSourceIntegrityTest.kt`
- `app/src/test/java/com/nbg/android/NbgDiagnosticsExportTest.kt`
- `app/src/test/java/com/nbg/android/AndroidManifestBehaviorTest.kt`

Acceptance:

- `skill-source-integrity-contract` defines trust boundary, source kinds, trust tiers, install policy, provenance schema, diagnostics redaction rules, and test oracle.
- Android source review blocks external plain `http` Skill URLs, blocks URL userinfo credentials, permits `https`, localhost `http`, and local absolute paths only as review-required sources.
- Downloaded URL Skill files get `nbg-skill-source-provenance-v1` metadata with file sha256, host, redacted source, and no raw URL query/token.
- Bundled default Skill auto-enable requires Android-pinned source asset size/SHA-256, matching server-pack `skills2set` bytes, and trusted bundled source classification; same-name user/external/path/unknown Skills cannot be preserved as default-enabled by name alone.
- Diagnostics export includes only aggregate Skill integrity counts and does not export Skill names, paths, descriptions, content, or full URLs.

Verification:

```bash
./gradlew --no-daemon :app:testDebugUnitTest --tests com.nbg.android.NbgSkillSourceIntegrityTest --tests com.nbg.android.NbgDiagnosticsExportTest --tests com.nbg.android.AndroidManifestBehaviorTest.skillSourceIntegrityContractIsDocumentedAndWired
./gradlew --no-daemon :app:testDebugUnitTest
```

Evidence:

- `NbgSkillSourceIntegrity.kt` introduces `NbgSkillSourceReview`, `NbgSkillSourceKind`, `NbgSkillTrustTier`, blocked/installable source review, summary trust inference, and provenance writing.
- `NbgSkillSourceIntegrity.kt` now defines `nbg-skill-source-integrity-v1`, pinned bundled Skill asset metadata for `nbg-engineering-core/SKILL.md`, bundled asset review, verified bundled Skill name selection, and `nbgMergeTrustedBundledSkillEnablement`.
- `HanakoServerLauncher.kt` seeds and default-enables only bundled Skills whose Android asset pins match; hash mismatch skips seeding, removes stale same-name seeded directories, and removes same-name untrusted bundled Skill entries from agent config.
- `HanakoApiClient.kt` now merges default enablement through `nbgMergeTrustedBundledSkillEnablement`, so an already-enabled same-name external/user/path Skill is removed instead of preserved.
- `HanakoChatController.kt` passes only locally verified bundled Skill names to `ensureAndroidBundledSkillsEnabled`.
- `HanakoSkillInstallSupport.kt` applies source review before network download and writes `.nbg-skill-provenance.json` after download validation.
- `NbgDiagnosticsExport.kt` reports `sourceIntegrityVersion`, expected bundled/pinned asset counts, `trustedBundledCount`, `userManagedCount`, `unverifiedExternalCount`, and `requiresReviewCount` without exporting Skill identifiers, hashes, paths, descriptions, content, URLs, or raw Skills API errors.
- `skill-source-integrity-contract.md` records pinned bundled asset rules, same-name untrusted default-enable removal, server-pack `skills2set` equivalence, safe Skills error diagnostics, state machine, endpoint semantics, security rules, diagnostics shape, and negative tests.
- Read-only Review Agent found three blocking gaps in the first implementation: same-name untrusted Skills could remain enabled, the server pack `skills2set` copy was not tied to the Android pinned asset, and raw Skills errors could leak names/hashes/paths through diagnostics. All three were fixed before final verification.
- `NbgSkillSourceIntegrityTest.bundledSkillAssetPinsMatchSourceAssets` verifies the Android pinned size/SHA-256 values match the source `SKILL.md`.
- `NbgSkillSourceIntegrityTest.bundledSkillHashMismatchBlocksTrustedDefaultEligibility` verifies tampered or missing bundled assets are not trusted default eligible.
- `NbgSkillSourceIntegrityTest.defaultEnablementDropsSameNameUntrustedBundledSkills` verifies an enabled same-name external Skill is removed while preserving unrelated user Skills and adding the verified bundled Skill.
- `NbgDiagnosticsExportTest` now verifies Skills diagnostics use `skills_error_present` and do not leak Skill names, 64-hex hashes, `/tmp` paths, `/opt` paths, private paths, or Skill content.
- `AndroidManifestBehaviorTest.bundledHanakoServerPackOnlyShipsNbgEngineeringCoreSkill` verifies the server pack's `skills2set/nbg-engineering-core/SKILL.md` bytes match the Android pinned default Skill asset.
- Targeted verification passes: `./gradlew --no-daemon :app:testDebugUnitTest --tests com.nbg.android.NbgSkillSourceIntegrityTest --tests com.nbg.android.NbgDiagnosticsExportTest --tests com.nbg.android.AndroidManifestBehaviorTest.skillSourceIntegrityContractIsDocumentedAndWired`.
- App unit verification passes: `./gradlew --no-daemon :app:testDebugUnitTest`.
- App debug APK builds: `./gradlew --no-daemon :app:assembleDebug`.
- Full release gate passes after Issue 13 changes: `./scripts/nbg_release_gate.sh`; latest summary at `build/release-gate/summary.txt`.
- Current targeted debug verification after bundled hash gate review fixes passes: `./gradlew --no-daemon :app:testDebugUnitTest --tests com.nbg.android.NbgSkillSourceIntegrityTest --tests com.nbg.android.NbgDiagnosticsExportTest --tests com.nbg.android.AndroidManifestBehaviorTest.skillSourceIntegrityContractIsDocumentedAndWired --tests com.nbg.android.AndroidManifestBehaviorTest.bundledHanakoServerPackOnlyShipsNbgEngineeringCoreSkill --tests com.nbg.android.AndroidManifestBehaviorTest.agentChatKeepsCoreHanakoProtocolAndDropsDeferredAdvancedFeatures --tests com.nbg.android.AndroidManifestBehaviorTest.androidExposesHanakoSkillsManagementControls`.
- Current full debug verification passes: `./gradlew --no-daemon :app:testDebugUnitTest :terminal-core:testDebugUnitTest :android-mcp-server:testDebugUnitTest :app:assembleDebug :android-mcp-server:assembleDebug`.
- Latest debug APK after bundled Skill hash gate changes: `app/build/outputs/apk/debug/app-debug.apk`, 228298272 bytes, SHA-256 `d37ec52e54500fc2d901487f659e8631b4cba6c54595d4c70abcbab6a9328458`, built `2026-07-05 11:16:12 +0000`.
- No lingering Gradle or Java process was present after verification.
- Release/R8 was intentionally not run for this issue per current build instruction.

- `NbgDiagnosticsExport.kt` reports `contextPolicyVersion`, `injectableLoadedCount`, `blockedSensitiveLoadedCount`, and `blockedTooLargeLoadedCount` without exporting raw Memory fields.

- `app/src/test/java/com/nbg/android/AndroidManifestBehaviorTest.kt`

Acceptance:

- `pet-resource-integrity-contract` documents participants, trust boundary, trust tiers, state machine, endpoint semantics, error semantics, compatibility, security rules, and test oracle.
- Android defines `nbg-pet-resource-integrity-v1` and writes `.nbg-pet-provenance.json` for bundled and PetDex installs.
- PetDex manifest, preview, and install downloads use HTTPS-only review, PetDex host policy, userinfo blocking, final redirect re-review, extension checks, and bounded streaming reads.
- PetDex sprite downloads are capped at 8 MiB, pet JSON at 512 KiB, and manifest at 1 MiB.
- PetDex installs stage files before committing and keep legacy PetDex installs usable as `unverified_petdex`.
- Diagnostics export includes only aggregate pet trust counts and never exports raw PetDex URLs, query strings, tokens, local paths, or file contents.

Verification:

```bash
./gradlew --no-daemon :app:testDebugUnitTest --tests com.nbg.android.NbgPetResourceIntegrityTest --tests com.nbg.android.NbgDiagnosticsExportTest --tests com.nbg.android.AndroidManifestBehaviorTest.petResourceIntegrityContractIsDocumentedAndWired --tests com.nbg.android.AndroidManifestBehaviorTest.androidExposesPetdexPetManagementControls --tests com.nbg.android.AndroidManifestBehaviorTest.petdexManifestParserSupportsSearchAndCharacterFiltering
./gradlew --no-daemon :app:testDebugUnitTest :app:assembleDebug
```

Evidence:

- `pet-resource-integrity-contract.md` defines `nbg-pet-resource-integrity-v1`, `trusted_bundled`, `unverified_petdex`, `blocked`, endpoint semantics, diagnostics shape, security rules, and test oracle.
- `NbgPetResourceIntegrity.kt` centralizes PetDex URL review, PetDex host policy, trust tiers, resource size limits, provenance schema, source review, and bounded byte reads.
- `NbgPetStore.kt` now reviews PetDex installs, downloads into a staging directory, re-reviews final redirect URLs, enforces sprite/JSON size caps, writes `.nbg-pet-provenance.json`, and commits staged files before updating prefs.
- `NbgPetDexClient.kt` bounds manifest downloads and reviews manifest URL/final URL.
- `NbgPetDexPreviewStore.kt` reviews preview sprite URLs, enforces the sprite size cap, and falls back to no preview instead of failing the whole PetDex dialog.
- `NbgDiagnosticsExport.kt` adds redacted aggregate pet resource integrity counts while preserving existing pet diagnostics fields.
- `NbgPetResourceIntegrityTest` covers allowed HTTPS PetDex assets, unsafe URL blocking, unsafe slug blocking, provenance redaction, legacy PetDex classification, bounded byte reads, and provenance presence checks.
- `AndroidManifestBehaviorTest.petResourceIntegrityContractIsDocumentedAndWired` locks contract/docs/source wiring.

## Issue 20: Terminal Readiness Capability Wiring

Owner Agent: Android Runtime Agent

Review Agent: QA/Release Agent

Security Review: not-required unless terminal output is exported beyond redacted diagnostics

Goal:

- Stop reporting Terminal as always healthy in the shared Capability Registry and consume live Terminal readiness snapshots for drawer and diagnostics health.

Files/Areas:

- `docs/issues/2026-07-04-001-m1-p0-issue-queue.md`
- `app/src/main/java/com/nbg/android/NbgCapabilityRegistry.kt`

- `app/src/test/java/com/nbg/android/AndroidManifestBehaviorTest.kt`

Acceptance:

- Terminal capability uses `TerminalReadinessSnapshot` values instead of a fixed healthy status.
- Any failed terminal readiness snapshot makes Terminal capability `Failed` with a short diagnostic in `lastError`.
- Any installing terminal readiness snapshot makes Terminal capability `Unknown` with `启动中` status.
- Ready terminal snapshots report a ready count while preserving the existing local fallback before any terminal has started.
- `NbgAgentUi.kt` passes `TerminalWorkspace.shared.terminalDiagnosticsSnapshot()` into the registry.
- Verification remains debug-only and does not run release/R8.


Evidence:

- `capability-registry-contract.md` now defines Terminal health from live `TerminalReadinessSnapshot` values and removes the old v2 open question.
- `NbgCapabilityRegistry.kt` now accepts `terminalReadiness`, derives Terminal `Healthy`/`Unknown`/`Failed`, reports ready count/startup state/failure state, and carries the short failed diagnostic in `lastError`.
- `NbgAgentUi.kt` passes `TerminalWorkspace.shared.terminalDiagnosticsSnapshot()` into the shared registry so drawer and diagnostics consume the same readiness source.
- `NbgCapabilityRegistryTest` covers ready, installing, and failed Terminal readiness aggregation.
- `AndroidManifestBehaviorTest.capabilityRegistryFeedsDrawerAndAgentSurfaces` locks source wiring for Terminal readiness input and Agent UI registry construction.

- `docs/issues/2026-07-04-001-m1-p0-issue-queue.md`
- `app/src/main/java/com/nbg/android/NbgAgentUi.kt`
- `app/src/main/java/com/nbg/android/NbgAgentFlushSchedulerState.kt`
- `app/src/test/java/com/nbg/android/NbgAgentFlushSchedulerStateTest.kt`
- `app/src/test/java/com/nbg/android/AndroidManifestBehaviorTest.kt`

Acceptance:

- `NbgAgentFlushSchedulerState` owns streaming-delta and tool-status `Job?` references.
- Scheduling streaming delta flush does not start a second active streaming flush job.
- Scheduling tool status flush does not start a second active tool flush job.
- Completing a flush clears the corresponding scheduler state and allows rescheduling.
- Cancelling a flush cancels the underlying Job and clears the corresponding scheduler state.
- `cancelAll` cancels and clears both flush Jobs.
- `NbgAgentUi.kt` no longer imports `Job` or declares direct `var deltaFlushJob/toolStatusFlushJob by remember` state.
- Streaming flush still delays by `HANA_MOBILE_STREAM_DELTA_FLUSH_MS` before `flushStreamingDeltas()`.
- Tool status flush still delays by `HANA_MOBILE_TOOL_STATUS_FLUSH_MS` before `flushToolStatuses()`.
- `flushStreamingDeltas()` and `flushToolStatuses()` clear scheduler state before calling `applyDeferredHistoryIfIdle()`.
- Immediate tool flush still cancels the pending tool flush before synchronous drain.
- `finishStreamingMessages()` still cancels streaming flush, flushes deltas, cancels tool flush, flushes tools, then marks remaining streaming/tool messages finished or failed.
- `clearConversationUi()` and `DisposableEffect` still cancel all pending flush Jobs.
- `replaceHistory()` cancels both streaming delta and tool status flush Jobs before clearing streaming/tool buffers.
- Verification remains debug-only and does not run release/R8.

Verification:

```bash
./gradlew --no-daemon :app:testDebugUnitTest --tests com.nbg.android.NbgAgentFlushSchedulerStateTest --tests com.nbg.android.AndroidManifestBehaviorTest.agentChatKeepsCoreHanakoProtocolAndDropsDeferredAdvancedFeatures --tests com.nbg.android.AndroidManifestBehaviorTest.agentRendersNativeWriteEditDiffAndTerminalToolCards
./gradlew --no-daemon :app:testDebugUnitTest :app:assembleDebug
```

Evidence:

- `NbgAgentFlushSchedulerState.kt` owns private streaming-delta and tool-status Job references, active-state checks, schedule, complete, cancel, and cancel-all operations.
- `NbgAgentUi.kt` now uses `val flushSchedulerState = remember { NbgAgentFlushSchedulerState() }` and no longer directly imports `Job` or declares `var deltaFlushJob/toolStatusFlushJob by remember`.
- `NbgAgentUi.kt` still owns `scope.launch`, delay constants, `flushStreamingDeltas()`, `flushToolStatuses()`, `applyDeferredHistoryIfIdle()`, and message mutation.
- `NbgAgentUi.kt` keeps streaming flush completion before deferred-history check and tool flush completion before deferred-history check.
- `NbgAgentUi.kt` keeps immediate tool flush cancellation before synchronous `flushToolStatuses()`.
- `NbgAgentUi.kt` keeps `finishStreamingMessages()` ordering: cancel streaming flush, flush deltas, cancel tool flush, flush tool statuses, then finalize live messages.
- Read-only Review Agent found a low-risk stale tool scheduler gap in `replaceHistory()`; `replaceHistory()` now cancels both streaming-delta and tool-status flush Jobs before clearing streaming/tool buffers.
- `NbgAgentFlushSchedulerStateTest` covers initial idle state, duplicate active scheduling prevention, complete/reschedule behavior, cancel behavior, and cancel-all behavior for both flush lanes.
- `AndroidManifestBehaviorTest.agentChatKeepsCoreHanakoProtocolAndDropsDeferredAdvancedFeatures` locks holder wiring, old direct Job state removal from `NbgAgentUi.kt`, scheduler cancel/complete/schedule calls, replace-history cancel ordering, finish-streaming ordering, and delay constants.
- Targeted debug verification passes after the review fix: `./gradlew --no-daemon :app:testDebugUnitTest --tests com.nbg.android.NbgAgentFlushSchedulerStateTest --tests com.nbg.android.AndroidManifestBehaviorTest.agentChatKeepsCoreHanakoProtocolAndDropsDeferredAdvancedFeatures --tests com.nbg.android.AndroidManifestBehaviorTest.agentRendersNativeWriteEditDiffAndTerminalToolCards`.
- App debug unit/build verification passes after one transient Gradle daemon startup exit and a clean rerun: `./gradlew --no-daemon :app:testDebugUnitTest :app:assembleDebug`.

## Issue 42: Bundled Skill Hash Gate

Status: code/contract/test complete. Android now treats bundled Skill default enablement as trusted only after pinned asset size/SHA-256 verification, server-pack `skills2set` byte equivalence, trusted bundled source classification, and same-name untrusted Skill removal. Diagnostics expose only aggregate Skill integrity counts and safe error presence. Verification remains debug-only and does not run release/R8.

Owner Agent: Security Agent + Agent Platform Agent

Review Agent: Security/QA Review Agent

Security Review: required before public beta with real device Skills state and seeded dummy external/user Skills

Goal:

- Close Issue 13's built-in Skill hash pin open question by preventing untrusted or tampered `nbg-engineering-core` content from being silently default-enabled as a trusted bundled Skill.

Files/Areas:

- `app/src/main/java/com/nbg/android/NbgSkillSourceIntegrity.kt`
- `app/src/main/java/com/nbg/android/HanakoServerLauncher.kt`
- `app/src/main/java/com/nbg/android/HanakoApiClient.kt`
- `app/src/main/java/com/nbg/android/HanakoChatController.kt`
- `app/src/main/java/com/nbg/android/NbgDiagnosticsExport.kt`
- `app/src/test/java/com/nbg/android/NbgSkillSourceIntegrityTest.kt`
- `app/src/test/java/com/nbg/android/NbgDiagnosticsExportTest.kt`
- `app/src/test/java/com/nbg/android/AndroidManifestBehaviorTest.kt`
- `docs/contracts/skill-source-integrity-contract.md`
- `docs/issues/2026-07-04-001-m1-p0-issue-queue.md`

Acceptance:

- Android source contains a pinned bundled Skill asset manifest with expected relative path, byte length, and SHA-256.
- Bundled Skill source review trusts `source=builtin` only for names in the Android pinned bundled Skill list and only when no file/external path fields indicate user-managed or external content.
- Launcher seeds and default-enables only bundled Skills whose Android pinned assets match; mismatch removes stale same-name seeded directories and does not add the Skill to default enabled config.
- Runtime API default enablement removes same-name untrusted bundled Skills instead of preserving them by name.
- Server pack `skills2set/nbg-engineering-core/SKILL.md` must match the Android pinned default Skill asset byte-for-byte.
- Diagnostics export exposes only aggregate Skill integrity fields and safe Skills error presence, not Skill names, hashes, paths, descriptions, source URLs, or raw Skills API error text.
- Verification remains debug-only and does not run release/R8.


Evidence:

- `NbgSkillSourceIntegrity.kt` defines `NBG_SKILL_SOURCE_INTEGRITY_VERSION = "nbg-skill-source-integrity-v1"`, `NbgBundledSkillAssetPin`, `NBG_ANDROID_BUNDLED_SKILL_ASSET_PINS`, `nbgReviewBundledSkillAssets`, `nbgVerifiedAndroidBundledSkillNames`, and `nbgMergeTrustedBundledSkillEnablement`.
- `NBG_ANDROID_BUNDLED_SKILL_ASSET_PINS` pins `nbg-engineering-core/SKILL.md` at 9719 bytes with SHA-256 `6ce4f8e231309558af0346f007447013b5122249adacc00adae0f0e23df00520`.
- `nbgSkillSummarySourceReview` now treats path-backed or external-path Skills as user/external before considering builtin trust, and builtin trust is limited to pinned bundled names.
- `HanakoServerLauncher.seedNbgDefaultSkills` reviews pinned bundled assets before copying; mismatch logs an error, removes stale same-name seeded directories, and excludes the Skill from default config enablement.
- `HanakoServerLauncher.ensureAndroidAgentEnabledSkills` removes bundled Skill names from agent config when they are not in the verified required list.
- `HanakoApiClient.ensureAndroidBundledSkillsEnabled` now calls `nbgMergeTrustedBundledSkillEnablement`, which removes enabled same-name untrusted bundled Skills while preserving unrelated user Skills.
- `HanakoChatController` computes verified bundled Skill names from Android assets before calling default enablement.
- `NbgDiagnosticsExport.kt` now reports `sourceIntegrityVersion`, `expectedBundledCount`, `pinnedBundledAssetCount`, trusted/user/unverified/review counts, and `skills_error_present` without exporting raw Skills API errors.
- `nbgRedactDiagnosticText` now redacts 64-hex SHA-256-like values and additional local path prefixes such as `/tmp`, `/opt`, `/usr`, `/var`, `/mnt`, and `/sdcard`.
- Read-only Review Agent found three initial gaps: same-name untrusted Skills could remain enabled, server-pack `skills2set` was not tied to the Android pinned asset, and raw Skills errors could leak names/hashes/paths. All were fixed and reverified.
- `NbgSkillSourceIntegrityTest.bundledSkillAssetPinsMatchSourceAssets` verifies pinned size/SHA values against the source asset.
- `NbgSkillSourceIntegrityTest.bundledSkillHashMismatchBlocksTrustedDefaultEligibility` verifies tampered or missing bundled assets fail trusted default eligibility.
- `NbgSkillSourceIntegrityTest.defaultEnablementDropsSameNameUntrustedBundledSkills` verifies same-name external Skill removal and verified bundled Skill addition.
- `NbgDiagnosticsExportTest` verifies Skill names, SHA-256-like strings, `/tmp` paths, `/opt` paths, private paths, Skill file paths, Memory content, MCP command values, and raw secrets are absent from diagnostics export.
- `AndroidManifestBehaviorTest.bundledHanakoServerPackOnlyShipsNbgEngineeringCoreSkill` verifies the server pack `skills2set` Skill bytes match the Android pinned default Skill asset.
- `skill-source-integrity-contract.md` now documents Android-pinned bundled assets, server-pack `skills2set` equivalence, same-name untrusted default-enable removal, and safe Skills error diagnostics.

- Full debug verification passes: `./gradlew --no-daemon :app:testDebugUnitTest :terminal-core:testDebugUnitTest :android-mcp-server:testDebugUnitTest :app:assembleDebug :android-mcp-server:assembleDebug`.

## Issue 44: Terminal Readiness Lifecycle Freshness

Status: code/contract/test complete for automated Terminal readiness freshness after leaving the Terminal page. `TerminalWorkspace` now owns long-lived session output observation, readiness changes expose an observable version consumed by Agent UI capability registry construction, stale session output cannot write into shifted tabs, and diagnostics export refreshes Terminal capability from the same readiness list used for Terminal counts. Verification remains debug-only and does not run release/R8.

Owner Agent: Android Runtime Agent + QA/Release Agent

Review Agent: Android Runtime Review Agent

Security Review: not-required unless terminal output is exported beyond redacted diagnostics

Goal:

- Close Issue 43's lifecycle freshness gap by ensuring Terminal capability health and diagnostics readiness stay current when terminal sessions continue producing output after the visible Terminal page is no longer composed.

Files/Areas:

- `app/src/main/java/com/nbg/android/TerminalWorkspace.kt`
- `app/src/main/java/com/nbg/android/TerminalReadinessController.kt`
- `app/src/main/java/com/nbg/android/MainActivity.kt`
- `app/src/main/java/com/nbg/android/NbgAgentUi.kt`
- `app/src/main/java/com/nbg/android/NbgCapabilityRegistry.kt`
- `app/src/main/java/com/nbg/android/NbgDiagnosticsExport.kt`
- `app/src/test/java/com/nbg/android/TerminalWorkspaceTest.kt`
- `app/src/test/java/com/nbg/android/TerminalReadinessControllerTest.kt`
- `app/src/test/java/com/nbg/android/NbgDiagnosticsExportTest.kt`
- `app/src/test/java/com/nbg/android/AndroidManifestBehaviorTest.kt`
- `docs/contracts/capability-registry-contract.md`
- `docs/issues/2026-07-04-001-m1-p0-issue-queue.md`

Acceptance:

- Terminal readiness output is analyzed and stored by `TerminalWorkspace` from each session's output flow, not only by the visible `TerminalScreen` Compose observer.
- `TerminalWorkspace.recordReadinessOutput` validates `TerminalSession` identity against the current session list before writing readiness/status, so stale output after tab deletion or movement cannot pollute another tab.
- Deleting or replacing a session cancels its readiness observer and clears its Ready-session tracking from `TerminalReadinessController`.
- Readiness snapshot changes increment a `readinessVersion` `StateFlow`; `NbgAgentUi` collects it and rebuilds the shared capability registry from fresh `terminalDiagnosticsSnapshot()` values.
- Diagnostics export refreshes any stale Terminal capability entry from the same readiness snapshot list used for `terminal.readyCount/installingCount/failedCount` and recent errors.
- Verification remains debug-only and does not run release/R8.

Verification:

```bash
./gradlew --no-daemon :app:testDebugUnitTest --tests com.nbg.android.TerminalWorkspaceTest --tests com.nbg.android.TerminalReadinessControllerTest --tests com.nbg.android.NbgDiagnosticsExportTest --tests com.nbg.android.AndroidManifestBehaviorTest.terminalReadinessIsTrackedForEverySession --tests com.nbg.android.AndroidManifestBehaviorTest.capabilityRegistryFeedsDrawerAndAgentSurfaces --tests com.nbg.android.AndroidManifestBehaviorTest.diagnosticsExportIsRedactedAndUserTriggeredFromDrawer
./gradlew --no-daemon :app:testDebugUnitTest :terminal-core:testDebugUnitTest :android-mcp-server:testDebugUnitTest :app:assembleDebug :android-mcp-server:assembleDebug
```

Evidence:

- `TerminalWorkspace` now has a single `TerminalReadinessController`, long-lived readiness observer jobs, and `readinessVersion: StateFlow<Int>`.
- `TerminalWorkspace.observeReadinessOutputFlow` collects session `screenText` and calls `recordReadinessOutput`; `setSession` registers this observer when a terminal session is installed.
- `recordReadinessOutput` resolves `TerminalSession` keys through `readinessIndexForSession`; stale `TerminalSession` output without a current session index is ignored instead of using a captured Compose index.
- `deleteSelectedTab` cancels the removed tab's readiness observer before removing aligned lists, clears ready-session tracking, removes the observer slot, and bumps readiness version.
- `setSession` clears prior ready-session tracking and cancels any prior observer before registering the replacement session.
- `TerminalReadinessController.clearSession` allows deleted/replaced ready sessions to stop being retained as permanently ready.
- `MainActivity.TerminalReadinessObservers` now delegates visible-page output to `workspace.recordReadinessOutput(...)` instead of owning an independent readiness controller.
- `NbgAgentUi` collects `terminalWorkspace.readinessVersion`, reads `terminalWorkspace.terminalDiagnosticsSnapshot()`, and passes that fresh list into `nbgBuildCapabilityRegistry`.
- `NbgDiagnosticsExport.kt` uses `withTerminalReadiness(snapshot.terminalReadiness)` so exported Terminal capability, Terminal counts, and recent errors agree even if a caller passes a stale registry.
- `TerminalWorkspaceTest.readinessOutputFlowUpdatesWorkspaceWithoutComposeObserver` proves a workspace-owned output flow updates readiness, tab status, and readiness version without a Compose observer.
- `TerminalWorkspaceTest.recordReadinessOutputKeepsReadyStateAcrossRecreatedObservers` proves the shared workspace controller prevents ready sessions from regressing when later output contains old failure text.
- `TerminalReadinessControllerTest.clearedSessionCanReportFailureAfterPreviouslyReady` proves ready-session state can be cleared for deleted/replaced sessions.
- `NbgDiagnosticsExportTest.diagnosticsExportRefreshesStaleTerminalCapabilityFromReadinessCounts` proves stale healthy Terminal capability export is replaced by fresh failed readiness evidence.
- `AndroidManifestBehaviorTest.terminalReadinessIsTrackedForEverySession`, `capabilityRegistryFeedsDrawerAndAgentSurfaces`, and `diagnosticsExportIsRedactedAndUserTriggeredFromDrawer` lock workspace observer ownership, readiness version consumption, session identity checks, and diagnostics capability refresh wiring.

- The follow-up implementation fixed those findings; second read-only Review Agent pass reported no High/blocking issues and no new explicit bugs.
- Targeted debug verification passes after review fixes: `./gradlew --no-daemon :app:testDebugUnitTest --tests com.nbg.android.TerminalWorkspaceTest --tests com.nbg.android.TerminalReadinessControllerTest --tests com.nbg.android.NbgDiagnosticsExportTest --tests com.nbg.android.AndroidManifestBehaviorTest.terminalReadinessIsTrackedForEverySession --tests com.nbg.android.AndroidManifestBehaviorTest.capabilityRegistryFeedsDrawerAndAgentSurfaces --tests com.nbg.android.AndroidManifestBehaviorTest.diagnosticsExportIsRedactedAndUserTriggeredFromDrawer`.

## Issue 45: Medium Terminal Command Confirmation Policy

Status: code/contract/test complete for the public-beta policy decision. Medium terminal/shell/command intents now require ordinary confirmation by default, without strong confirmation unless a Dangerous pattern is detected. Backend-declared `riskTier`/`risk` values may raise risk but cannot downgrade Android's local inference. The bundled Hanako runtime approval wrapper is covered by a source oracle. Verification remains debug-only and does not run release/R8.

Owner Agent: Security Agent + Agent Platform Agent

Review Agent: Security/QA Review Agent

Security Review: required before public beta device UI sign-off

Goal:

- Close Issue 19's open Medium terminal-command policy question by making the safer public-beta default explicit in code, contract, tests, and issue evidence.

Files/Areas:

- `app/src/main/java/com/nbg/android/NbgPermissionRiskModel.kt`
- `app/src/test/java/com/nbg/android/HanakoBridgeTest.kt`
- `app/src/test/java/com/nbg/android/AndroidManifestBehaviorTest.kt`
- `docs/contracts/permission-risk-model-contract.md`
- `docs/issues/2026-07-04-001-m1-p0-issue-queue.md`

Acceptance:

- Medium terminal/shell/command risk requires ordinary confirmation by default.
- Medium terminal/shell/command risk does not require strong confirmation unless the command is Dangerous.
- Low risk remains non-blocking, High remains ordinary confirmation, and Dangerous remains strong confirmation.
- Backend-declared risk values may raise but must not lower Android's local inference.
- Local inference includes top-level confirmation fields plus subject and payload fields from bundled `tool_action_approval` events.
- Destructive shell commands remain Dangerous even when declared as Medium or Low.
- PetDex/plugin/external resource/MCP side effects remain High even when declared as Medium or Low.
- Bundled Hanako runtime still owns the production approval gate and emits `tool_action_approval` / `session_confirmation` before side-effect tools execute in `ask` mode.
- Issue 19 no longer carries Medium terminal-command behavior as an unresolved policy decision.
- Verification remains debug-only and does not run release/R8.

```bash
./gradlew --no-daemon :app:testDebugUnitTest :terminal-core:testDebugUnitTest :android-mcp-server:testDebugUnitTest :app:assembleDebug :android-mcp-server:assembleDebug
```

- `NbgPermissionRiskTier.Medium` now sets `requiresConfirmation = true` and `strongConfirmation = false`.
- `nbgPermissionRiskForConfirmationBlock` now combines Android local inference with backend-declared `riskTier`/`risk` by choosing the highest tier, preventing backend declarations from downgrading destructive or external-resource side effects.
- Android local inference now includes `payload.toolName` and selected `payload.params` values such as `command`, `url`, `path`, `file_path`, `action`, `target`, `label`, and `key`.
- `HanakoBridgeTest.permissionRiskModelClassifiesSideEffects` asserts Low read-like operations remain non-confirming while ordinary shell commands require non-strong confirmation.
- `HanakoBridgeTest.mediumTerminalCommandsRequireConfirmationWithoutStrongConfirmation` proves ordinary terminal commands and declared Medium confirmation blocks require ordinary confirmation without strong confirmation.
- `HanakoBridgeTest.declaredRiskCannotDowngradeDestructiveTerminalCommand` proves a declared Medium `rm -rf` command remains Dangerous and strong-confirmed.
- `HanakoBridgeTest.declaredRiskCannotDowngradeExternalResourceOrMcpSideEffects` proves declared Low/Medium PetDex install, plugin install, and MCP tool calls remain High.
- `HanakoBridgeTest.payloadRiskCannotDowngradeBundledConfirmationShape` proves bundled-style `payload: { toolName, params }` confirmations cannot downgrade destructive terminal commands or external resource installs.
- `permission-risk-model-contract.md` documents the Medium terminal policy, the non-downgrade rule, and the bundled Hanako approval-generation boundary.

The follow-up implementation fixed both with highest-tier merge logic, downgrade tests, contract language, and bundled runtime approval-wrapper assertions.

The follow-up implementation fixed both with payload risk text, bundled-style payload tests, allowlist/side-effect set assertions, and tar entry size/read completeness checks.

## Issue 46: Operate Mode Session Warning Gate

Status: code/contract/test complete for the public-beta `operate` mode warning decision. `operate` remains user-selectable, but first use in each active session now requires a session-level warning acceptance. Stored `operate` preferences do not auto-apply to new/restored sessions until accepted; Android displays and syncs `ask` until the warning is accepted. Verification remains debug-only and does not run release/R8.

Owner Agent: Security Agent + UX/UI Agent

Review Agent: Security/QA Review Agent

Security Review: required before public beta device UI sign-off

Goal:

- Close the Permission Risk Model open question on whether `operate` requires a session-level warning before first use, choosing the safer public-beta default and preventing stored preferences from bypassing that warning.

Files/Areas:

- `app/src/main/java/com/nbg/android/NbgAgentConfirmationState.kt`
- `app/src/main/java/com/nbg/android/NbgAgentDialogs.kt`
- `app/src/main/java/com/nbg/android/NbgAgentUi.kt`
- `app/src/main/java/com/nbg/android/NbgAgentComposerUi.kt`
- `app/src/test/java/com/nbg/android/NbgAgentConfirmationStateTest.kt`
- `app/src/test/java/com/nbg/android/AndroidManifestBehaviorTest.kt`
- `docs/contracts/permission-risk-model-contract.md`
- `docs/issues/2026-07-04-001-m1-p0-issue-queue.md`

Acceptance:

- Selecting `operate` from the composer opens a session-level warning before the mode is saved or sent to Hanako.
- Accepting the warning applies `operate` for the current session and records that session as accepted.
- Dismissing the warning keeps the current session gated and leaves the UI/backend in `ask`.
- Accepted and dismissed warning state is tracked per session, so switching sessions does not overwrite another session's acceptance or dismissal state.
- A stored `operate` preference cannot auto-apply to a new/restored session until the warning is accepted for that session.
- If backend/session state is already `operate` without local session acceptance, Android attempts to sync the session back to `ask`.
- The permission mode sync effect reruns when backend permission mode changes or streaming ends so unaccepted backend `operate` cannot remain active and deferred preference sync/gating is not skipped.
- Contract and source tests prove the warning gate, stored-preference behavior, and removal of the `operate` open question.
- Verification remains debug-only and does not run release/R8.

Verification:

```bash
./gradlew --no-daemon :app:testDebugUnitTest --tests com.nbg.android.NbgAgentConfirmationStateTest --tests com.nbg.android.AndroidManifestBehaviorTest.permissionRiskModelContractIsDocumentedAndWired --tests com.nbg.android.AndroidManifestBehaviorTest.agentChatKeepsCoreHanakoProtocolAndDropsDeferredAdvancedFeatures --tests com.nbg.android.HanakoBridgeTest.permissionDefaultsStartInAskModeInsteadOfOperate --tests com.nbg.android.HanakoBridgeTest.permissionRiskModelClassifiesSideEffects --tests com.nbg.android.HanakoBridgeTest.mediumTerminalCommandsRequireConfirmationWithoutStrongConfirmation --tests com.nbg.android.HanakoBridgeTest.payloadRiskCannotDowngradeBundledConfirmationShape
./gradlew --no-daemon :app:testDebugUnitTest :terminal-core:testDebugUnitTest :android-mcp-server:testDebugUnitTest :app:assembleDebug :android-mcp-server:assembleDebug
```

Evidence:

- `NbgAgentConfirmationState` now tracks `operatePermissionWarningOpen`, the pending warning session path, accepted session key set, dismissed session key set, and exposes `shouldGateOperatePermissionMode` / `shouldAutoPromptOperatePermissionWarning`.
- `NbgOperatePermissionModeDialog` warns that `operate` allows more active side-effect execution and that unaccepted sessions remain in `ask`.
- `NbgAgentUi` routes composer permission changes through `requestPreferredPermissionMode`; gated `operate` opens the warning instead of saving or applying the mode.
- `NbgAgentUi` displays `ask` while a stored `operate` preference is unaccepted for the current session.
- The preference sync effect blocks unaccepted stored `operate`, auto-prompts once per session unless dismissed, forces backend `operate` back to `ask` when needed, and includes both `hanakoState.permissionMode` and `hanakoState.streaming` in its keys so backend mode changes and streaming completion rerun sync/gating.
- `NbgAgentComposerUi` now labels `operate` as requiring first-use confirmation and updates the `ask` subtitle to cover Medium+ risk confirmation.
- `permission-risk-model-contract.md` documents the `operate` session warning rule, stored-preference non-bypass rule, and removes the `operate` warning open question.
- `NbgAgentConfirmationStateTest.operatePermissionWarningGatesOperatePerSessionUntilAccepted` proves per-session gating, dismiss behavior, manual re-request, and accepted-session isolation across multiple accepted sessions.
- `NbgAgentConfirmationStateTest.operatePermissionWarningDismissalIsTrackedPerSession` proves a prompt/dismiss in one session does not clear another session's dismissal state.
- `AndroidManifestBehaviorTest.agentChatKeepsCoreHanakoProtocolAndDropsDeferredAdvancedFeatures` locks the UI/source wiring for request/apply helpers, stored `operate` display fallback, auto-prompt logic, forced `ask` sync, backend permission-mode key, streaming key, and dialog.
- `AndroidManifestBehaviorTest.permissionRiskModelContractIsDocumentedAndWired` locks the contract/source oracle for the new `operate` warning decision.
- First read-only Review Agent pass found no blocker, one High issue where `hanakoState.permissionMode` was missing from sync effect keys, and one Medium issue where accepted/dismissed warning state only stored one session key. The follow-up implementation fixed both with per-session key sets, additional multi-session tests, and explicit `hanakoState.permissionMode` source oracle coverage.
- Second read-only Review Agent pass reported no blocker and no High/Medium/Low findings; prior High and Medium findings were confirmed fixed.
- Targeted debug verification passes: `./gradlew --no-daemon :app:testDebugUnitTest --tests com.nbg.android.NbgAgentConfirmationStateTest --tests com.nbg.android.AndroidManifestBehaviorTest.permissionRiskModelContractIsDocumentedAndWired --tests com.nbg.android.AndroidManifestBehaviorTest.agentChatKeepsCoreHanakoProtocolAndDropsDeferredAdvancedFeatures --tests com.nbg.android.HanakoBridgeTest.permissionDefaultsStartInAskModeInsteadOfOperate --tests com.nbg.android.HanakoBridgeTest.permissionRiskModelClassifiesSideEffects --tests com.nbg.android.HanakoBridgeTest.mediumTerminalCommandsRequireConfirmationWithoutStrongConfirmation --tests com.nbg.android.HanakoBridgeTest.payloadRiskCannotDowngradeBundledConfirmationShape`.

## Issue 47: Backend Confirmation Risk Schema Merge

Status: code/contract/test complete for the backend confirmation risk schema decision. Confirmation events may now carry optional backend risk metadata under `risk`, `riskSchema`, or `permissionRisk`; Android treats that schema as advisory UI/risk input that may raise risk and supply target/recovery detail, while local Android inference remains the minimum accepted tier. Verification remains debug-only and does not run release/R8.

Owner Agent: Security Agent + Agent Platform Agent

Review Agent: Security/QA Review Agent

Security Review: required before public beta device confirmation UI sign-off

Goal:

- Close the Permission Risk Model open question on whether confirmation events should carry backend-provided risk schema, while preserving Android's local non-downgrade safety invariant.

Files/Areas:

- `app/src/main/java/com/nbg/android/NbgPermissionRiskModel.kt`
- `app/src/test/java/com/nbg/android/HanakoBridgeTest.kt`
- `app/src/test/java/com/nbg/android/AndroidManifestBehaviorTest.kt`
- `docs/contracts/permission-risk-model-contract.md`
- `docs/issues/2026-07-04-001-m1-p0-issue-queue.md`

Acceptance:

- Android supports optional backend risk schema objects under `risk`, `riskSchema`, or `permissionRisk`.
- Supported schema fields include `tier`/`riskTier`/`level`/`risk`, `severity`, `action`, `toolName`/`tool`/`name`, `command`, `target`/`targetLabel`/`path`/`url`/`resource`, `reason`/`summary`/`description`, and `recoveryHint`/`recovery`/`mitigation`/`hint`.
- Backend schema may raise local risk and provide target/recovery detail for confirmation UI.
- Backend schema must never downgrade local inference; destructive shell commands remain Dangerous and external resource/MCP side effects remain High or above.
- Schema action/tool/command/reason fields are classified as a separate raise-only candidate and cannot lower Android's conservative default for sparse confirmation blocks.
- Backend recovery hints are ignored when their declared/schema risk is lower than Android's local inference.
- Existing top-level `riskTier`, `risk`, and `severity` compatibility remains.
- The permission-risk contract no longer has an open question for backend-provided risk schema.
- Verification remains debug-only and does not run release/R8.

Verification:

```bash
./gradlew --no-daemon :app:testDebugUnitTest --tests com.nbg.android.HanakoBridgeTest.backendRiskSchemaCanRaiseRiskAndProvideRecoveryHint --tests com.nbg.android.HanakoBridgeTest.backendRiskSchemaCannotDowngradeLocalInference --tests com.nbg.android.HanakoBridgeTest.declaredRiskCannotDowngradeDestructiveTerminalCommand --tests com.nbg.android.HanakoBridgeTest.payloadRiskCannotDowngradeBundledConfirmationShape --tests com.nbg.android.HanakoBridgeTest.confirmationParserAddsRiskAndRecoveryHints --tests com.nbg.android.AndroidManifestBehaviorTest.permissionRiskModelContractIsDocumentedAndWired
./gradlew --no-daemon :app:testDebugUnitTest :terminal-core:testDebugUnitTest :android-mcp-server:testDebugUnitTest :app:assembleDebug :android-mcp-server:assembleDebug
```

Evidence:

- `NbgPermissionRiskModel.kt` now resolves a backend risk schema object from `riskSchema`, object-shaped `risk`, or `permissionRisk`.
- `nbgPermissionDeclaredRiskTier` merges top-level severity/risk fields with schema tier/severity values and selects the highest declared tier before combining it with Android local inference.
- `nbgPermissionRiskTierForWire` accepts `danger`/`dangerous`/`critical`/`destructive`, `high`/`elevated`, `medium`/`moderate`, and `low` wire values.
- `nbgPermissionRiskForSchema` classifies schema action/tool/command/reason fields as a separate raise-only candidate; schema text no longer participates in Android's local minimum inference.
- Schema target fields can populate `targetLabel` when subject/top-level target is absent, and schema recovery fields can populate the confirmation recovery hint only when the schema/top-level backend risk is not lower than local inference.
- `HanakoBridgeTest.backendRiskSchemaCanRaiseRiskAndProvideRecoveryHint` proves schema can raise a read-like action to High and supply target/recovery detail through `parseHanakoConfirmationBlock`.
- `HanakoBridgeTest.backendRiskSchemaCannotDowngradeLocalInference` proves schema-declared Low cannot downgrade a sparse confirmation's conservative Medium default, a destructive terminal command, a plugin install, or an MCP tool call; it also proves weak schema recovery text cannot replace the local Dangerous recovery hint.
- Existing downgrade tests for top-level declarations and bundled payload shape remain in place.
- `permission-risk-model-contract.md` documents backend risk schema fields, advisory/raise-only semantics, and now lists no Month 1 public-beta open questions for the permission risk model entry point.
- `AndroidManifestBehaviorTest.permissionRiskModelContractIsDocumentedAndWired` locks the schema contract text, parser helper names, and new test names.
- First read-only Review Agent pass found no blocker/High issues, but found Medium gaps where schema text could lower sparse-block inference and low-risk schema hints could override local Dangerous guidance. ## Issue 48: FTP Public Beta Read-Only Mode

Status: code/contract/test complete for explicit FTP access modes. File Share now starts FTP in `ReadOnly` mode for public beta by default, exposes an explicit `ReadWrite` switch, rejects write-class FTP commands before filesystem mutation while read-only, and exports the current access mode in diagnostics. Verification remains debug-only and does not run release/R8.

Owner Agent: Security Agent + Runtime Reliability Agent

Review Agent: Security/QA Review Agent

Security Review: required before public beta device sign-off

Goal:

- Close the local-service-boundary open question on FTP read-only mode by making public-beta File Share default to read-only while preserving an explicit authenticated read/write path.

Files/Areas:

- `app/src/main/java/com/nbg/android/NbgFileShareModel.kt`
- `app/src/main/java/com/nbg/android/NbgFtpFileServer.kt`
- `app/src/main/java/com/nbg/android/NbgAgentFileShareUi.kt`
- `app/src/main/java/com/nbg/android/NbgAgentUi.kt`
- `app/src/main/java/com/nbg/android/NbgFileShareService.kt`
- `app/src/main/java/com/nbg/android/NbgDiagnosticsExport.kt`
- `app/src/test/java/com/nbg/android/NbgFtpFileServerTest.kt`
- `app/src/test/java/com/nbg/android/NbgDiagnosticsExportTest.kt`
- `app/src/test/java/com/nbg/android/AndroidManifestBehaviorTest.kt`
- `docs/contracts/local-service-boundary-contract.md`
- `docs/issues/2026-07-04-001-m1-p0-issue-queue.md`

Acceptance:

- FTP access mode is represented explicitly as `ReadOnly` or `ReadWrite`.
- Opening File Share from the app starts FTP in `ReadOnly` mode by default.
- The File Share dialog displays current access mode and offers explicit `ReadOnly` / `ReadWrite` switching.
- `ReadOnly` mode still allows authenticated directory listing, metadata, and file retrieval.
- `ReadOnly` mode rejects `STOR`, `APPE`, `DELE`, `RMD`/`XRMD`, `MKD`/`XMKD`, `RNFR`, and `RNTO` before filesystem mutation.
- Closing FTP or switching access modes cancels pending passive transfers so an old `ReadWrite` session cannot complete a delayed write after the server is closed or replaced by `ReadOnly`.
- `ReadWrite` mode preserves the existing authenticated upload, rename, mkdir, and delete behavior.
- Diagnostics export includes the FTP `accessMode` wire value without exporting password or host root.
- `local-service-boundary-contract.md` documents the public-beta FTP read-only default and no longer asks whether FTP should add read-only mode.
- Verification remains debug-only and does not run release/R8.

Verification:

```bash
./gradlew --no-daemon :app:testDebugUnitTest --tests com.nbg.android.NbgFtpFileServerTest.closingServerCancelsPendingPassiveStoreBeforeFilesystemMutation --tests com.nbg.android.NbgFtpFileServerTest.readOnlyModeAllowsListingAndRetrievalButRejectsWrites --tests com.nbg.android.NbgFtpFileServerTest.ftpLoginListStoreRetrieveRenameAndDeleteWork --tests com.nbg.android.NbgFtpFileServerTest.storeWithoutDataConnectionDoesNotCreateParentDirectories --tests com.nbg.android.NbgDiagnosticsExportTest --tests com.nbg.android.AndroidManifestBehaviorTest.appLocalServicesStayLoopbackOnlyWithTokenOrPasswordBoundary --tests com.nbg.android.AndroidManifestBehaviorTest.appStartsInAgentChatShellAndKeepsTerminalAndServices
./gradlew --no-daemon :app:testDebugUnitTest :terminal-core:testDebugUnitTest :android-mcp-server:testDebugUnitTest :app:assembleDebug :android-mcp-server:assembleDebug
```

Evidence:

- `NbgFileShareAccessMode` defines `ReadOnly` and `ReadWrite` wire/display values plus the write-allowance predicate.
- `NbgFileShareServerState` carries `accessMode`; registry snapshots and diagnostics snapshots preserve it without exposing credentials.
- `NbgFileShareServerRegistry.start(context, accessMode)` defaults to `ReadOnly`, reuses an existing server only when the requested mode matches, and restarts the loopback FTP server when switching modes.
- `NbgAgentUi.openFileShareDialog()` starts File Share with `NbgFileShareAccessMode.ReadOnly`.
- `NbgFileShareDialog` displays the current mode and exposes explicit `ReadOnly` and `ReadWrite` actions.
- `NbgFtpFileServer.ensureWriteAllowed()` closes any pending passive socket, clears pending rename state, and rejects write-class commands with `550 FTP server is read-only`.
- `NbgFtpFileServer` tracks active passive sockets, closes them during server shutdown, and rechecks `running` after passive data accept so a closed/replaced server cannot continue into filesystem mutation.
- `NbgFileShareService` notification/log output includes current access mode.
- `NbgDiagnosticsExport` exports FTP `accessMode` while continuing to avoid raw password and host-root export.
- `NbgFtpFileServerTest.readOnlyModeAllowsListingAndRetrievalButRejectsWrites` proves authenticated listing/retrieval still work and write-class commands do not create, delete, rename, or append files.
- `NbgFtpFileServerTest.closingServerCancelsPendingPassiveStoreBeforeFilesystemMutation` proves a delayed `STOR` waiting on passive data cannot create parent directories or files after server close/access-mode replacement.
- `NbgFtpFileServerTest.ftpLoginListStoreRetrieveRenameAndDeleteWork` proves the explicit read/write path preserves existing FTP behavior.
- `NbgDiagnosticsExportTest` proves diagnostics include `read_write` mode while still redacting FTP secrets.
- `AndroidManifestBehaviorTest.appLocalServicesStayLoopbackOnlyWithTokenOrPasswordBoundary` locks the read-only default, explicit mode switching, write gate, diagnostics accessMode field, and removal of the FTP read-only open question.
- `AndroidManifestBehaviorTest.appStartsInAgentChatShellAndKeepsTerminalAndServices` locks File Share UI wiring for default `ReadOnly`, refresh in current mode, and explicit `ReadWrite` switch.
- First read-only Review Agent pass found one High issue: a delayed passive `STOR` from an old `ReadWrite` session could still complete after switching to `ReadOnly` because passive sockets were not closed by server shutdown. The follow-up implementation fixed this with tracked passive socket closure, a post-accept `running` check, contract/source-oracle coverage, and a pending-passive-store regression test.
- Second read-only Review Agent pass reported no blocker and no High/Medium/Low findings; the prior High finding was confirmed fixed.
- Targeted debug verification passes: `./gradlew --no-daemon :app:testDebugUnitTest --tests com.nbg.android.NbgFtpFileServerTest.closingServerCancelsPendingPassiveStoreBeforeFilesystemMutation --tests com.nbg.android.NbgFtpFileServerTest.readOnlyModeAllowsListingAndRetrievalButRejectsWrites --tests com.nbg.android.NbgFtpFileServerTest.ftpLoginListStoreRetrieveRenameAndDeleteWork --tests com.nbg.android.NbgFtpFileServerTest.storeWithoutDataConnectionDoesNotCreateParentDirectories --tests com.nbg.android.NbgDiagnosticsExportTest --tests com.nbg.android.AndroidManifestBehaviorTest.appLocalServicesStayLoopbackOnlyWithTokenOrPasswordBoundary --tests com.nbg.android.AndroidManifestBehaviorTest.appStartsInAgentChatShellAndKeepsTerminalAndServices`.
- Full debug verification passes: `./gradlew --no-daemon :app:testDebugUnitTest :terminal-core:testDebugUnitTest :android-mcp-server:testDebugUnitTest :app:assembleDebug :android-mcp-server:assembleDebug`.

Status: code/contract/test complete for the Hanako server-info diagnostic export decision. Owner Agent: Security Agent + QA/Release Agent

Review Agent: Security/QA Review Agent

Security Review: required before public beta diagnostics sign-off

Goal:

- Close the local-service-boundary open question on Hanako server-info diagnostic export by locking default redaction and summary-only launcher diagnostics.

- `docs/contracts/diagnostics-export-contract.md`

Acceptance:

- Diagnostics `excluded` list includes `raw_hanako_server_info`.
- Launcher diagnostics export only `serverInfoPresent`, `pidPresent`, `versionPresent`, log-present booleans, and redacted log tails.
- Launcher diagnostics do not export raw server-info JSON or raw `token`, `port`, `pid`, or `version` values from `server-info.json`.
- Launcher/server log tails redact server-info-derived `pid=...`, `port=...`, `version=...`, npm lifecycle `name@version` banners, `already running on ...`, `observed healthy server on ...`, `http://127.0.0.1:<port>`, `http://localhost:<port>`, and `listening on :<port>` values.
- Local service boundary contract states that raw Hanako `server-info.json` is not exported by default.
- Diagnostics export contract documents Hanako launcher/server-info derived booleans only, lists raw Hanako server-info as excluded, and consistently allows only bounded redacted launcher/server log tails.

- Verification remains debug-only and does not run release/R8.

```bash
./gradlew --no-daemon :app:testDebugUnitTest --tests com.nbg.android.NbgDiagnosticsExportTest --tests com.nbg.android.AndroidManifestBehaviorTest.diagnosticsExportIsRedactedAndUserTriggeredFromDrawer --tests com.nbg.android.AndroidManifestBehaviorTest.appLocalServicesStayLoopbackOnlyWithTokenOrPasswordBoundary

```

Evidence:

- `NbgDiagnosticsExport.kt` adds `raw_hanako_server_info` to the explicit excluded-data list.
- `NbgDiagnosticsLauncherSnapshot.toDiagnosticsJson()` continues to export only `serverInfoPresent`, `pidPresent`, `versionPresent`, `launchLogPresent`, `serverLogPresent`, and redacted `launchLogTail` / `serverLogTail`.
- `nbgRedactDiagnosticText()` now redacts server-info-derived `pid=...`, `port=...`, `version=...`, `already running on ...`, and `observed healthy server on ...` patterns before launcher/server log tails are exported.
- `nbgRedactDiagnosticText()` redacts npm lifecycle `name@version` banners so source fallback `npm run server` output cannot leak the Hanako package version from server logs.
- `nbgRedactDiagnosticText()` also redacts loopback URL port forms (`http://127.0.0.1:<port>`, `http://localhost:<port>`) and `listening on :<port>` forms observed in bundled Hanako server logs.
- `safeDiagnosticTail()` now reads only the final 64 KiB of launcher/server diagnostic logs before selecting the last lines, so log tail export is bounded in memory and content.
- `NbgDiagnosticsExportTest.diagnosticsExportIncludesStateCountsAndCapabilityHealth` verifies the excluded list contains `raw_hanako_server_info` and launcher JSON does not contain raw `serverInfo`, `serverInfoJson`, `rawServerInfo`, `token`, `port`, `pid`, or `version` fields.
- `NbgDiagnosticsExportTest.diagnosticsExportRedactsSecretsPathsAndUserContent` and `diagnosticTextRedactsCommonTokenForms` verify launcher/server log tails do not leak concrete `pid`, `port`, or `version` values from Hanako server-info-derived log lines.
- `AndroidManifestBehaviorTest.diagnosticsExportIsRedactedAndUserTriggeredFromDrawer` locks the diagnostics source shape, contract language, and removed local-service open question.
- `local-service-boundary-contract.md` now states diagnostics must not export raw Hanako `server-info.json` by default and may expose only derived booleans.
- `diagnostics-export-contract.md` now documents Hanako launcher/server-info derived booleans only, lists raw Hanako server-info in excluded data, and clarifies that bounded launcher/server log tails are allowed only after shared diagnostic redaction.
- First read-only Review Agent pass found no blocker/High issues, but found a Medium gap where log tails could still leak server-info-derived `port`/`pid` values and a Low contract inconsistency about whether v1 reads log tails. The follow-up implementation fixed both with log-tail server-info value redaction, JSON/redactor/source-oracle coverage, and corrected contract language.

- Third read-only Review Agent pass found one Medium gap where source fallback `npm run server` output could leak the Hanako package version as `name@version`. The follow-up implementation fixed it with npm lifecycle banner redaction and regression/source-oracle coverage.
- Final read-only Review Agent pass found one Medium stability issue where `safeDiagnosticTail()` bounded output but still read full log files, plus one Low test gap for direct `localhost:<port>` coverage. The follow-up implementation fixed both with a 64 KiB bounded tail reader, localhost redaction regression coverage, and source-oracle coverage banning full `readLines()` tail extraction.

## Issue 51: Stream Resume Diagnostics Counters

Status: code/contract/test/review complete for stream-resume diagnostic counters. Android now exports bounded stream-resume counters and cursor summary only, without raw replay events, stream ids, or session paths. Verification remains debug-only and does not run release/R8.

Owner Agent: Agent Platform Agent + QA/Release Agent

Review Agent: Security/QA Review Agent

Security Review: required before public beta diagnostics sign-off

Goal:

- Close the Android Hanako event contract open question on whether Android should expose stream-resume counters in diagnostics without recording raw event data.

Files/Areas:

- `app/src/main/java/com/nbg/android/HanakoBridge.kt`
- `app/src/main/java/com/nbg/android/HanakoChatController.kt`
- `app/src/main/java/com/nbg/android/NbgDiagnosticsExport.kt`
- `app/src/test/java/com/nbg/android/NbgDiagnosticsExportTest.kt`
- `app/src/test/java/com/nbg/android/AndroidManifestBehaviorTest.kt`
- `docs/contracts/android-hanako-event-contract.md`
- `docs/issues/2026-07-04-001-m1-p0-issue-queue.md`

Acceptance:

- Android records stream-resume request count and received resume count.
- Android records replayed, accepted, skipped, and duplicate replay-event counts without storing raw replay payloads.
- Android records truncated/reset resume counts, cursor-present boolean, and last accepted sequence number.
- Diagnostics export includes only these numeric/boolean cursor fields under `hanako.streamResume`.
- Diagnostics export does not include stream-resume `sessionPath`, `streamId`, or raw `events`.
- Existing stream filtering behavior remains: reset still clears active turn state, same-stream duplicate seq replay is skipped, new stream ids reset sequence tracking, and accepted replay events update stream metadata before payload handling.
- Wrong-session replay events are counted as skipped and do not enter accepted replay counts or update stream metadata.
- `android-hanako-event-contract.md` documents the allowed stream-resume diagnostics and the prohibited raw event/session/stream export.
- The Hanako event contract no longer asks whether Android should expose stream-resume counters in diagnostics.
- Verification remains debug-only and does not run release/R8.

Verification:

```bash
./gradlew --no-daemon :app:testDebugUnitTest --tests com.nbg.android.AndroidManifestBehaviorTest.agentChatKeepsCoreHanakoProtocolAndDropsDeferredAdvancedFeatures --tests com.nbg.android.NbgDiagnosticsExportTest --tests com.nbg.android.NbgHanakoEventContractTest --tests com.nbg.android.AndroidManifestBehaviorTest.androidHanakoEventContractIsDocumentedAndWired --tests com.nbg.android.AndroidManifestBehaviorTest.diagnosticsExportIsRedactedAndUserTriggeredFromDrawer
./gradlew --no-daemon :app:testDebugUnitTest :terminal-core:testDebugUnitTest :android-mcp-server:testDebugUnitTest :app:assembleDebug :android-mcp-server:assembleDebug
```

Evidence:

- `HanakoStreamResumeDiagnostics` stores only counts, `cursorPresent`, and `lastSeq`; it has no `sessionPath`, `streamId`, event array, or raw event payload fields.
- `HanakoChatState` carries `streamResumeDiagnostics` so diagnostics can read a stable aggregate instead of inspecting raw WebSocket frames.
- `HanakoChatController.requestStreamResume()` increments `requestCount` before sending `resume_stream`.
- `HanakoChatController.handleStreamResume()` increments received resume, replayed, accepted, skipped, duplicate, truncated, and reset counters while preserving reset/truncated handling and accepted-event metadata updates.
- `HanakoChatController.handleStreamResume()` now gates replay subevents through `isCurrentSessionMessage(event)` before skip-reason and metadata updates so wrong-session replay payloads count as skipped instead of accepted.
- `streamEventSkipReason()` centralizes same-stream duplicate sequence detection and keeps `shouldAcceptStreamEvent()` behavior compatible for live events.
- `NbgDiagnosticsExport` writes `hanako.streamResume` with `requestCount`, `resumeCount`, `replayedEventCount`, `acceptedReplayEventCount`, `skippedReplayEventCount`, `duplicateReplayEventCount`, `truncatedResumeCount`, `resetResumeCount`, `cursorPresent`, and `lastSeq`.
- `NbgDiagnosticsExportTest.diagnosticsExportIncludesStateCountsAndCapabilityHealth` verifies the exported stream-resume fields and asserts the export omits `sessionPath`, `streamId`, and `events`.
- `AndroidManifestBehaviorTest.androidHanakoEventContractIsDocumentedAndWired` locks the contract text, removed open question, stream-resume request wiring, and diagnostics recorder wiring.
- `AndroidManifestBehaviorTest.diagnosticsExportIsRedactedAndUserTriggeredFromDrawer` locks the diagnostics export field names for the stream-resume aggregate.
- `AndroidManifestBehaviorTest.agentChatKeepsCoreHanakoProtocolAndDropsDeferredAdvancedFeatures` was updated to lock the new skip-reason flow while preserving reset-first, wrong-session skip, and duplicate-seq behavior.
- `android-hanako-event-contract.md` now allows stream-resume counters without raw event data, states skipped replay counts include duplicate/wrong-session replay events, and explicitly prohibits raw stream-resume event payloads, stream ids, or session paths in diagnostics.
- Read-only Security/QA Review Agent pass reported no blocker and no High/Medium/Low findings; it confirmed the diagnostics export is counters/booleans/`lastSeq` only, raw replay/session/stream data is absent, and `handleStreamResume()` preserves reset/truncated/duplicate/accepted-event ordering.
