## Issue 52: Android MCP Beta Local Bearer Token

Status: code/contract/test/review complete for Android MCP beta bearer authentication. The standalone MCP beta server now requires a locally generated bearer token for `/mcp` GET/POST/DELETE even while tools remain read-only/echo-only. Verification remains debug-only and does not run release/R8.

Owner Agent: Security Agent + Android MCP Agent

Review Agent: Security/QA Review Agent

Security Review: required before public beta MCP sign-off

Goal:

- Close the local-service-boundary open question on whether Android MCP beta should require a local bearer token even while tools remain read-only.
- Close the android-mcp-server contract open question on whether the standalone MCP server should require a local bearer token before non-beta tools are added.

Files/Areas:

- `android-mcp-server/src/main/java/com/nbg/android/mcpserver/AndroidMcpServerContract.kt`
- `android-mcp-server/src/main/java/com/nbg/android/mcpserver/McpServerService.kt`
- `android-mcp-server/src/main/java/com/nbg/android/mcpserver/MainActivity.kt`
- `android-mcp-server/src/test/java/com/nbg/android/mcpserver/McpServerBoundaryTest.kt`
- `app/src/test/java/com/nbg/android/AndroidManifestBehaviorTest.kt`
- `docs/contracts/local-service-boundary-contract.md`
- `docs/contracts/android-mcp-server-contract.md`
- `docs/issues/2026-07-04-001-m1-p0-issue-queue.md`

Acceptance:

- MCP server generates a local bearer token on first service start and persists it in the standalone MCP app's private preferences with app backup disabled.
- `/mcp` `GET`, `POST`, and `DELETE` require `Authorization: Bearer {token}` before opening SSE, handling JSON-RPC, or closing a session.
- Missing, malformed, short, wrong-scheme, wrong-token, or extra-suffix bearer headers are rejected.
- Unauthorized MCP requests return HTTP 401 with a `WWW-Authenticate: Bearer realm="NBG Android MCP"` challenge.
- The token is not included in foreground notification text, server info, tool responses, diagnostics contract evidence, or source-oracle evidence.
- MCP server UI exposes user-initiated copy and regenerate token actions and shows only token presence in status text.
- Regenerated tokens take effect for subsequent `/mcp` requests without restarting the server.
- Copied bearer tokens are marked clipboard-sensitive where Android supports it and are cleared after a short TTL only when Android reports no primary-clip change after the token copy; the clear path must not read clipboard contents.
- MCP running state is persisted only after socket bind succeeds; startup failure and service destruction write `running=false`, with startup failure detail preserved as last error until the next successful bind.
- Unexpected MCP accept-loop failures propagate to the service failure path instead of being treated as normal shutdown.
- Existing beta tools remain limited to `android_device_info` and `android_echo`.
- `local-service-boundary-contract.md` documents bearer authentication and no longer asks whether Android MCP beta should require a token.
- `android-mcp-server-contract.md` documents bearer authentication and no longer asks whether the standalone MCP server should require a token before non-beta tools.
- Verification remains debug-only and does not run release/R8.

Verification:

```bash
./gradlew --no-daemon :android-mcp-server:testDebugUnitTest --tests com.nbg.android.mcpserver.McpServerBoundaryTest :app:testDebugUnitTest --tests com.nbg.android.AndroidManifestBehaviorTest.appLocalServicesStayLoopbackOnlyWithTokenOrPasswordBoundary --tests com.nbg.android.AndroidManifestBehaviorTest.androidMcpServerContractIsDocumentedAndWired
./gradlew --no-daemon :app:testDebugUnitTest :terminal-core:testDebugUnitTest :android-mcp-server:testDebugUnitTest :app:assembleDebug :android-mcp-server:assembleDebug
```

Evidence:

- `AndroidMcpServerContract.kt` now defines bearer auth constants and `nbgAndroidMcpRequestAuthorized()`.
- `nbgAndroidMcpRequestAuthorized()` accepts exact `Authorization: Bearer {token}` matches and rejects missing, wrong, wrong-scheme, extra-suffix, and short expected-token cases.
- `McpServerService.startServer()` calls `ensureBearerToken(this)` and starts `AndroidMcpHttpServer` with a `bearerTokenProvider` that reads the current private preference token.
- `AndroidMcpHttpServer.handle()` now checks `nbgAndroidMcpRequestAuthorized(headers, bearerTokenProvider())` after `/mcp` path validation and before method dispatch.
- Unauthorized `/mcp` requests call `writeUnauthorized()` and return HTTP 401 with `WWW-Authenticate: Bearer realm="NBG Android MCP"`.
- `McpServerService` stores `KEY_BEARER_TOKEN`, `KEY_BEARER_TOKEN_PRESENT`, and startup `KEY_LAST_ERROR` in private preferences; `allowBackup` and `fullBackupContent` remain false in the MCP manifest.
- `McpServerService` writes `running=true` only through the socket bind `onListening` callback; startup failure and service destruction write `running=false`, and `onDestroy()` preserves existing startup failure detail.
- `AndroidMcpHttpServer.start()` now treats `stopped=true` as the only normal accept-loop shutdown condition; other accept failures propagate to `McpServerService` so `markServerStopped(error.message)` can run.
- `McpServerService` notification text says bearer auth is required but does not include the token value; it shows `Starting` before bind success and switches to `Listening` only from the bind-success callback.
- `MainActivity` adds `Copy Bearer Token` and `Regenerate Bearer Token` user actions, writes copied token through Android clipboard only on tap, marks the clip with `ClipDescription.EXTRA_IS_SENSITIVE` on Android 13+, clears the clip after 30 seconds when unchanged, displays `Token present: yes/no` instead of rendering the raw token in status text, and tells running clients to update the header after regeneration.
- `McpServerBoundaryTest.bearerAuthorizationRequiresExactLocalToken` directly tests auth-header semantics.
- `McpServerBoundaryTest.mcpHttpServerRejectsUnauthenticatedRequestsBeforeProtocolHandling` starts a real loopback test server and proves unauthenticated `GET`, `POST`, and `DELETE` return 401 before SSE, JSON-RPC, or 204 handling; it also proves authorized `POST initialize` still succeeds.
- `McpServerBoundaryTest.mcpHttpServerReadsCurrentBearerTokenForEveryRequest` proves old tokens fail and the new token works after provider token rotation without restarting the test server.
- `McpServerBoundaryTest.mcpHttpServerPropagatesUnexpectedAcceptFailure` proves a non-`stop()` accept failure stops the server thread by propagating the exception.
- `McpServerBoundaryTest.mcpServerServiceIsPrivateLoopbackOnlyAndReadOnlyBeta` locks loopback binding, bearer gate wiring, token persistence keys, 401 challenge, token generation, and notification non-leakage.
- `McpServerBoundaryTest.androidMcpServerContractIsDocumentedAndWired` locks bearer auth contract constants and service wiring.
- `AndroidManifestBehaviorTest.appLocalServicesStayLoopbackOnlyWithTokenOrPasswordBoundary` locks MCP bearer auth in the P0 local-service boundary.
- `AndroidManifestBehaviorTest.androidMcpServerContractIsDocumentedAndWired` locks standalone MCP contract/source/test/UI bearer-token wiring.
- `local-service-boundary-contract.md` now requires Android MCP beta bearer authentication and lists no Month 1 public-beta local service authentication open questions.
- `android-mcp-server-contract.md` now requires bearer authentication and removes the open question about requiring a token before non-beta tools.
- First read-only Security/QA Review Agent pass found no blocker/High issues, but found three Medium issues and one Low test gap: token rotation could leave an old startup-captured token alive, `running=true` was written before bind success and failure could be misreported, clipboard token copy lacked sensitive/TTL handling, and source oracles did not prove runtime GET/POST/DELETE rejection. The follow-up implementation fixed all four with dynamic bearer-token reads, bind-success running-state persistence, preserved startup last-error state, sensitive/TTL clipboard handling, and real loopback socket tests.
- Second read-only Security/QA Review Agent pass confirmed the three Medium issues and original Low were fixed, then found one residual Low where unexpected `accept()` failures could be swallowed as normal shutdown. The follow-up implementation fixed it by propagating non-`stopped` accept failures and adding a debug regression test.
- Final read-only Security/QA Review Agent pass reported no blocker and no High/Medium/Low findings; it confirmed the residual Low was fixed, APK hashes matched, and release/R8 was not run.
- Targeted debug verification passes: `./gradlew --no-daemon :android-mcp-server:testDebugUnitTest --tests com.nbg.android.mcpserver.McpServerBoundaryTest :app:testDebugUnitTest --tests com.nbg.android.AndroidManifestBehaviorTest.appLocalServicesStayLoopbackOnlyWithTokenOrPasswordBoundary --tests com.nbg.android.AndroidManifestBehaviorTest.androidMcpServerContractIsDocumentedAndWired`.
- Full debug verification passes: `./gradlew --no-daemon :app:testDebugUnitTest :terminal-core:testDebugUnitTest :android-mcp-server:testDebugUnitTest :app:assembleDebug :android-mcp-server:assembleDebug`.
- Latest debug APK after Issue 52 changes: `app/build/outputs/apk/debug/app-debug.apk`, 228318109 bytes, SHA-256 `0aa41939e16b7f9ee54e0dd6b49dabe1a1a481e540187135e0dcc44d14716fe9`, built `2026-07-05 20:39:40 +0000`.
- MCP debug APK after Issue 52 changes: `android-mcp-server/build/outputs/apk/debug/android-mcp-server-debug.apk`, 2166158 bytes, SHA-256 `6e19c608371ba1eb96294ccafe2112c1506353cd97631ff8182dea49e489c4da`, built `2026-07-05 21:17:21 +0000`.
- No lingering Gradle, Java, or Kotlin process was present after verification.
- Release/R8 was intentionally not run for this issue per current build instruction.

Remaining work before public beta:

- Run a device-level MCP smoke test: confirm `/mcp` returns 401 without the header, succeeds with copied token, and old token fails after regeneration without restarting the server.

## Issue 53: Memory Write Local Audit Metadata

Status: code/contract/test/review complete for local Memory write audit metadata. Android now records bounded local audit metadata for Memory create/update/delete attempts without storing raw Memory content, title, tags, ids, source session, source turn id, query, error text, or content hashes. Verification remains debug-only and does not run release/R8.

Owner Agent: Security Agent + Agent Platform Agent

Review Agent: Security/QA Review Agent

Security Review: required before public beta Memory sign-off

Goal:

- Close the Memory context contract open question on whether Memory writes should create a local audit event without storing raw content.

Files/Areas:

- `app/src/main/java/com/nbg/android/HanakoMemoryModels.kt`
- `app/src/main/java/com/nbg/android/HanakoChatController.kt`
- `app/src/main/java/com/nbg/android/NbgCapabilityRegistry.kt`
- `app/src/main/java/com/nbg/android/NbgDiagnosticsExport.kt`
- `app/src/test/java/com/nbg/android/NbgCapabilityRegistryTest.kt`
- `app/src/test/java/com/nbg/android/NbgMemoryContextPolicyTest.kt`
- `app/src/test/java/com/nbg/android/NbgDiagnosticsExportTest.kt`
- `app/src/test/java/com/nbg/android/AndroidManifestBehaviorTest.kt`
- `docs/contracts/memory-context-contract.md`
- `docs/issues/2026-07-04-001-m1-p0-issue-queue.md`

Acceptance:

- Memory create/update attempts record local audit metadata with action, result, normalized type, policy risk, policy version, and timestamp only.
- Memory delete attempts record local audit metadata with action/result/policy version/timestamp only and never store the deleted item id.
- Policy-blocked writes record `result=blocked`; successful writes/deletes record `result=success`; local/API failures record `result=failure`.
- Audit events are bounded to `NBG_MEMORY_AUDIT_MAX_EVENTS`; diagnostics exports only the most recent `NBG_MEMORY_AUDIT_DIAGNOSTIC_TAIL_LIMIT` events.
- Diagnostics exports Memory audit metadata only: `action`, `result`, `normalizedType`, `risk`, `policyVersion`, and `timestampMillis`.
- Diagnostics never exports Memory content, title, tags, item id, source session, source turn id, search query, raw error text, or hashes of private content from audit events.
- Diagnostics surfaces Memory API error presence only as `memory_error_present` across `memory.error`, `hanako.lastError`, `capabilities[].lastError`, and `recentErrors`.
- Loading Memory state preserves the local audit tail instead of wiping it.
- `memory-context-contract.md` documents local audit metadata and no longer asks whether Memory writes should create a local audit event without raw content.
- Verification remains debug-only and does not run release/R8.

Verification:

```bash
./gradlew --no-daemon :app:testDebugUnitTest --tests com.nbg.android.NbgCapabilityRegistryTest --tests com.nbg.android.NbgMemoryContextPolicyTest --tests com.nbg.android.NbgDiagnosticsExportTest --tests com.nbg.android.AndroidManifestBehaviorTest.memoryContextContractIsDocumentedAndWired
./gradlew --no-daemon :app:testDebugUnitTest :terminal-core:testDebugUnitTest :android-mcp-server:testDebugUnitTest :app:assembleDebug :android-mcp-server:assembleDebug
```

Evidence:

- `HanakoMemoryAuditEvent` stores only action/result/type/risk/policy/timestamp metadata.
- `nbgMemoryAuditEvent()` normalizes unknown action/result values to `unknown`, normalizes type/risk to known allowlists, and clamps negative timestamps to zero.
- `HanakoMemoryState.withMemoryAuditEvent()` keeps a bounded tail of `NBG_MEMORY_AUDIT_MAX_EVENTS`.
- `HanakoChatController.saveMemoryItem()` records blocked create/update audit events before returning policy errors.
- `HanakoChatController.runMemoryMutation()` records success/failure audit events around local Hanako Memory API mutations while preserving audit tail across refreshed Memory state.
- `HanakoChatController.runMemoryMutation()` tracks `memoryMutationCompleted` so a successful write followed by a Memory state reload failure records the write audit result as `success` while still surfacing the reload error to UI.
- `HanakoChatController.deleteMemoryItem()` records delete audit metadata through a fixed `delete` action and does not put the deleted id into audit fields.
- `HanakoChatController.loadMemoryState()` preserves existing audit events when replacing Memory items from Hanako.
- `NbgDiagnosticsExport` adds `memory.auditEventCount` and bounded `memory.recentAuditEvents`.
- `HanakoMemoryAuditEvent.toDiagnosticsJson()` exports only action/result/normalizedType/risk/policyVersion/timestampMillis and never raw Memory fields.
- `NbgDiagnosticsExport` now exports Memory errors as fixed `memory_error_present` summaries in `memory.error`, `hanako.lastError`, `capabilities[].lastError`, and `recentErrors`, so raw Memory API errors cannot leak query, item id, title/content/tag echoes, or path fragments.
- `NbgCapabilityRegistry` stores both Memory capability errors and Memory-origin Hanako capability errors as `memory_error_present` rather than raw backend text.
- `NbgCapabilityRegistryTest.registrySummarizesHanakoCapabilityWhenBackendErrorComesFromMemory` verifies `nbgBuildCapabilityRegistry(lastError = RAW_MEMORY_ERROR, memoryError = RAW_MEMORY_ERROR)` does not retain Memory query, item id, title, tag, or raw backend error text in capability errors.
- `NbgMemoryContextPolicyTest.memoryAuditEventsKeepOnlyMetadataAndStayBounded` verifies normalization and bounded retention.
- `NbgDiagnosticsExportTest.diagnosticsExportIncludesStateCountsAndCapabilityHealth` verifies audit diagnostics fields, `hanako.lastError`, Memory capability `lastError`, `recentErrors`, and absence of content/title/tags/id/source/query fields.
- `NbgDiagnosticsExportTest.diagnosticsExportSanitizesRegistryBuiltHanakoCapabilityMemoryErrors` verifies registry-built Hanako and Memory capability errors export as `memory_error_present` and capability-derived `recentErrors` do not include raw Memory API fields.
- `NbgDiagnosticsExportTest.diagnosticsExportRedactsSecretsPathsAndUserContent` seeds a raw Memory API error containing query/id/title/tag text and verifies diagnostics export does not contain those values.
- `AndroidManifestBehaviorTest.memoryContextContractIsDocumentedAndWired` locks model/controller/diagnostics/contract wiring and confirms the open question was removed.
- `memory-context-contract.md` now documents local audit metadata, allowed audit fields, diagnostics export rules, and prohibited raw audit payload fields.
- Targeted debug verification passes: `./gradlew --no-daemon :app:testDebugUnitTest --tests com.nbg.android.NbgMemoryContextPolicyTest --tests com.nbg.android.NbgDiagnosticsExportTest --tests com.nbg.android.AndroidManifestBehaviorTest.memoryContextContractIsDocumentedAndWired`.
- Full debug verification passes: `./gradlew --no-daemon :app:testDebugUnitTest :terminal-core:testDebugUnitTest :android-mcp-server:testDebugUnitTest :app:assembleDebug :android-mcp-server:assembleDebug`.
- First read-only Security/QA Review Agent pass found no blocker/High issues, but found one Medium gap where `memory.error` and `recentErrors` could export raw Memory API error text containing query, item id, or backend-echoed Memory fields. The follow-up implementation fixed it with a fixed `memory_error_present` diagnostics summary and regression assertions.
- Second read-only Security/QA Review Agent pass found one additional Medium gap where raw Memory API errors could still export via `hanako.lastError`, Memory `capabilities[].lastError`, and capability-derived `recentErrors`. The second follow-up fixed those paths with `toDiagnosticsLastError()` helpers, Memory capability summary storage, and regression assertions.
- Third read-only Security/QA Review Agent pass found the actual registry-generated Hanako capability path could still inherit Memory-origin raw `lastError`. The third follow-up fixed `nbgBuildCapabilityRegistry()` with `hanakoLastError` summary logic and added registry-built export regression coverage.
- Final read-only Security/QA Review Agent pass found no Blocker, High, Medium, or Low issues and confirmed the registry-built Hanako capability Memory raw error path is closed.
- Latest debug APK after Issue 53 changes: `app/build/outputs/apk/debug/app-debug.apk`, 228323188 bytes, SHA-256 `62d427a294ed3d45bd2aeb8659dfa50af7b9afaabcc4f79de365653af2ccdcb6`, built `2026-07-05 21:58:20 +0000`.
- MCP debug APK unchanged by Issue 53: `android-mcp-server/build/outputs/apk/debug/android-mcp-server-debug.apk`, 2166158 bytes, SHA-256 `6e19c608371ba1eb96294ccafe2112c1506353cd97631ff8182dea49e489c4da`, built `2026-07-05 21:17:21 +0000`.
- No lingering Gradle, Java, or Kotlin process was present after verification.
- Release/R8 was intentionally not run for this issue per current build instruction.

Remaining work before public beta:

- Run a device Memory add/edit/delete smoke test and inspect diagnostics JSON to confirm only audit metadata appears.

## Issue 56: Manual Skill Install And Enable Review Gate

Status: code/contract/test/review complete for manual Skill install and enable review gate. The Skills page now requires explicit source review before installing manual path/URL Skills, requires confirmation before enabling review-required Skills, and the controller prevents newly installed or same-name replaced unverified/user-managed Skills from remaining enabled if Hanako auto-enables them. Verification remains debug-only and does not run release/R8.

Owner Agent: Security Agent + UX/UI Agent

Review Agent: Security Review Agent

Security Review: required before public beta Skills sign-off

Goal:

- Close the Skill source integrity contract open question on whether manual Skills page installation should require an explicit review dialog before enabling instead of relying on Hanako post-install enable state.

Files/Areas:

- `app/src/main/java/com/nbg/android/NbgSkillSourceIntegrity.kt`
- `app/src/main/java/com/nbg/android/HanakoChatController.kt`
- `app/src/main/java/com/nbg/android/NbgAgentSkillsUi.kt`
- `app/src/test/java/com/nbg/android/NbgSkillSourceIntegrityTest.kt`
- `app/src/test/java/com/nbg/android/AndroidManifestBehaviorTest.kt`
- `docs/contracts/skill-source-integrity-contract.md`
- `docs/issues/2026-07-04-001-m1-p0-issue-queue.md`

Acceptance:

- Manual Skills page install for path/URL sources shows source review before install.
- Source review shows source kind, trust tier, redacted location/host, allow/block result, and review state without exposing raw URL query or local path values.
- Review-required Skills require explicit confirmation before manual enablement.
- If Hanako auto-enables a newly installed review-required Skill, Android restores the enabled list so new unverified/user-managed Skills remain disabled until user confirmation.
- Previously enabled review-required Skills are preserved during a later manual install.
- Same-name replacements do not inherit enabled state when source kind, trust tier, review requirement, or path identity changes.
- `skill-source-integrity-contract.md` documents manual install/enable review behavior and removes the open question.
- Verification remains debug-only and does not run release/R8.

```bash
./gradlew --no-daemon :app:testDebugUnitTest :terminal-core:testDebugUnitTest :android-mcp-server:testDebugUnitTest :app:assembleDebug :android-mcp-server:assembleDebug
```

- `nbgEnabledSkillsAfterManualInstall()` keeps previously enabled Skills only when the after-install Skill review identity still matches, and drops newly enabled or same-name replaced review-required Skills after manual install.
- `nbgSkillReviewIdentity()` includes source kind, trust tier, review requirement, source string, and path identity to prevent name-only enabled-state inheritance.
- `NbgSkillSourceIntegrityTest.manualInstallEnablementDropsNewReviewRequiredSkills` covers preservation of previously enabled review-required Skills and dropping newly auto-enabled user/external Skills.

## Issue 58: Confirmation Resolution Timeline Card

Status: code/contract/test/review complete for compact confirmation resolution timeline cards. `confirmation_resolved` events now leave a local compact confirmation card in the chat timeline after the pending dialog closes, preserving the approval/rejection result, risk label, target, and recovery hint for auditability, including duplicate local HTTP/WebSocket resolution delivery. Verification remains debug-only and does not run release/R8.

Owner Agent: UX/UI Agent + Agent Platform Agent

Review Agent: UX/QA Review Agent

Security Review: lightweight review required because confirmation cards expose permission risk, target, and recovery context; no new side effects or diagnostics export fields are introduced

Goal:

- Close the tool visualization contract open question on whether confirmation resolved/rejected events should produce a compact timeline card after resolution.

Files/Areas:

- `app/src/main/java/com/nbg/android/NbgToolVisualizationEventContract.kt`
- `app/src/main/java/com/nbg/android/NbgToolPresentation.kt`
- `app/src/main/java/com/nbg/android/NbgAgentUi.kt`
- `app/src/test/java/com/nbg/android/NbgToolVisualizationEventContractTest.kt`
- `app/src/test/java/com/nbg/android/AndroidManifestBehaviorTest.kt`
- `docs/contracts/tool-visualization-event-contract.md`
- `docs/issues/2026-07-04-001-m1-p0-issue-queue.md`

Acceptance:

- `confirmation_resolved` produces a visible compact local timeline card after the pending confirmation dialog resolves.
- Confirmed actions normalize to `kind=confirmation`, `status=succeeded`, and `success=true`.
- Rejected actions normalize to `kind=confirmation` and `status=cancelled` without pretending a tool execution failed or succeeded.
- The initial card used a stable `confirmation:<confirmId>` key and `session_confirmation` tool name; Issue 64 later hardened current keys to `confirmation:<confirmIdHashPrefix>`.
- The card preserves bounded risk label, subject label, target, and recovery hint while avoiding confirmation body/tool argument expansion.
- Duplicate local HTTP and WebSocket `confirmation_resolved` delivery keeps the latest resolution state while preserving previously captured risk, target, and recovery context.
- The message UI labels confirmation cards as `确认` instead of generic `工具调用`.
- `tool-visualization-event-contract.md` documents the behavior and removes the resolved open question.
- Verification remains debug-only and does not run release/R8.

Verification:

```bash
./gradlew --no-daemon :app:testDebugUnitTest --tests com.nbg.android.NbgToolVisualizationEventContractTest --tests com.nbg.android.AndroidManifestBehaviorTest.toolVisualizationEventContractIsDocumentedAndWired
./gradlew --no-daemon :app:testDebugUnitTest :terminal-core:testDebugUnitTest :android-mcp-server:testDebugUnitTest :app:assembleDebug :android-mcp-server:assembleDebug
```

Evidence:

- `nbgConfirmationResolutionToolStatus()` converts `confirmed` to `succeeded`/`success=true` and `rejected` to `cancelled`; Issue 64 later hardened the stable key from raw confirmation id to `confirmation:<confirmIdHashPrefix>`.
- The helper stores only compact risk/subject/target/recovery metadata and does not include the confirmation body or tool arguments.
- `NbgAgentUi` enqueues the compact timeline card before `confirmationState.resolveConfirmation(event.confirmId)` clears the pending dialog state.
- `NbgAgentUiLogic.mergeConfirmationToolStatus()` prevents duplicate resolution delivery from overwriting a rich confirmation card with a generic fallback card.
- `NbgToolPresentation.nbgToolKindLabel()` labels `confirmation` cards as `确认`.
- `NbgToolVisualizationEventContractTest.confirmationResolutionProducesCompactTimelineStatus` covers stable key, kind, tool name, state, visible status, risk, target, recovery hint, and confirmed/rejected mapping.
- `NbgToolVisualizationEventContractTest.duplicateConfirmationResolutionMergePreservesAuditContext` covers HTTP/WebSocket duplicate resolution merge and asserts confirmation body/tool args do not enter title, subtitle, or detail.
- `AndroidManifestBehaviorTest.toolVisualizationEventContractIsDocumentedAndWired` locks contract text, helper wiring, UI event wiring, display label, and open-question removal.
- `tool-visualization-event-contract.md` now documents compact confirmation timeline card semantics and removes the resolved open question.

## Issue 59: Diagnostics Export FileProvider Attachment Share

Status: code/contract/test/review complete for diagnostics export attachment sharing. Diagnostics Export sharing now writes the redacted JSON into app cache and sends it through a non-exported `FileProvider` URI instead of putting the full JSON into `Intent.EXTRA_TEXT`. Verification remains debug-only and does not run release/R8.

Owner Agent: QA/Release Agent + Security Agent

Review Agent: Security/QA Review Agent

Security Review: required because diagnostics export crosses the Android share boundary and must not expose broader file paths or unredacted JSON text

Goal:

- Close the diagnostics export contract open question on whether v2 should write an attachment file through `FileProvider` instead of text share.

Files/Areas:

- `app/src/main/java/com/nbg/android/NbgDiagnosticsExportShare.kt`
- `app/src/main/java/com/nbg/android/NbgAgentUi.kt`
- `app/src/main/AndroidManifest.xml`
- `app/src/main/res/xml/diagnostics_file_paths.xml`
- `app/src/test/java/com/nbg/android/NbgDiagnosticsExportTest.kt`
- `app/src/test/java/com/nbg/android/AndroidManifestBehaviorTest.kt`
- `docs/contracts/diagnostics-export-contract.md`
- `docs/issues/2026-07-04-001-m1-p0-issue-queue.md`

Acceptance:

- Diagnostics Export Share writes the already-redacted JSON to `cache/diagnostics/nbg-diagnostics-<timestamp>.json`.
- The share `Intent` uses `ACTION_SEND`, `application/json`, `Intent.EXTRA_STREAM`, `ClipData.newUri`, and `FLAG_GRANT_READ_URI_PERMISSION`.
- The share path is exposed only through a non-exported `androidx.core.content.FileProvider` rooted at `cache-path path="diagnostics/"`.
- The share intent no longer puts the full JSON into `Intent.EXTRA_TEXT`.
- Old `nbg-diagnostics-*.json` cache attachments are pruned before writing the newest attachment.
- Copy action remains available for users who need raw redacted JSON text.
- Attachment write/share failures show a recoverable toast and do not crash the UI.
- `diagnostics-export-contract.md` documents FileProvider attachment sharing and removes the resolved open question.
- Verification remains debug-only and does not run release/R8.

Verification:

```bash
./gradlew --no-daemon :app:testDebugUnitTest --tests com.nbg.android.NbgDiagnosticsExportTest --tests com.nbg.android.AndroidManifestBehaviorTest.diagnosticsExportIsRedactedAndUserTriggeredFromDrawer
./gradlew --no-daemon :app:testDebugUnitTest :terminal-core:testDebugUnitTest :android-mcp-server:testDebugUnitTest :app:assembleDebug :android-mcp-server:assembleDebug
```

Evidence:

- `NbgDiagnosticsExportShare.kt` builds the diagnostics share `Intent` with `FileProvider.getUriForFile()`, `Intent.EXTRA_STREAM`, `ClipData.newUri`, and `Intent.FLAG_GRANT_READ_URI_PERMISSION`.
- `NbgDiagnosticsExportShare.kt` writes attachments under `context.cacheDir/diagnostics` and prunes old `nbg-diagnostics-*.json` files before writing the newest redacted JSON attachment.
- `NbgAgentUi.shareDiagnosticsExport()` calls `nbgBuildDiagnosticsExportShareIntent(context, diagnosticsExportState.exportText)` inside `runCatching`; missing share targets still show `没有可用的分享目标`, and attachment/share failures show `诊断导出分享失败`.
- `AndroidManifest.xml` registers a non-exported `androidx.core.content.FileProvider` at `${applicationId}.diagnostics` with `grantUriPermissions=true`.
- `diagnostics_file_paths.xml` exposes only the app cache `diagnostics/` directory for diagnostics attachments.
- `NbgDiagnosticsExportTest.diagnosticsExportAttachmentFileWriterCreatesJsonAttachmentAndPrunesOldExports` verifies attachment filename, cache directory placement, file content, and old attachment pruning.
- `AndroidManifestBehaviorTest.diagnosticsExportIsRedactedAndUserTriggeredFromDrawer` locks helper wiring, `EXTRA_STREAM`, ClipData, read grant, FileProvider Manifest config, cache path config, direct absence of `Intent.EXTRA_TEXT` from `NbgDiagnosticsExportShare.kt`, removal of full JSON `EXTRA_TEXT` from `NbgAgentUi.kt`, and contract open-question removal.
- `diagnostics-export-contract.md` now documents FileProvider attachment sharing, cache-rooted attachment path, read-grant rule, and `EXTRA_TEXT` avoidance.
- Initial read-only Security/QA Review Agent pass found one Low issue: tests only forbade the old UI callsite text-share expression and did not directly lock that the share helper itself avoids `Intent.EXTRA_TEXT`. Follow-up added the direct source oracle assertion.
- Final read-only Security/QA Review Agent pass found no Blocker, High, Medium, or Low issues and confirmed FileProvider, attachment URI, and `EXTRA_TEXT` boundaries are locked.
- Targeted debug verification passes: `./gradlew --no-daemon :app:testDebugUnitTest --tests com.nbg.android.NbgDiagnosticsExportTest --tests com.nbg.android.AndroidManifestBehaviorTest.diagnosticsExportIsRedactedAndUserTriggeredFromDrawer`.

Status: code/contract/test/review complete for structured launcher failure codes. Diagnostics Export now includes `launcher.failureCodes` as bounded enum strings derived from safe launcher state and redacted launcher/server log patterns, while preserving redacted log tails and forbidding raw pid, port, version, token, path, or log text inside the codes. ## Issue 62: Pet Resource User Managed Trust Tier

Status: code/contract/test/review complete for separating user-managed Pet resources from blocked unknown sources and unverified PetDex resources. Pet resource integrity now has a `user_managed` source/trust tier, diagnostics export reports only normalized source kinds and aggregate counts, and blank or spoofed source labels do not become trusted or PetDex by default. Verification remains debug-only and does not run release/R8.

Owner Agent: Security Agent + UX/UI Agent

Review Agent: Security/QA Review Agent

Security Review: required because Pet source classification controls trust tier, diagnostics export, and beta external resource boundaries

Goal:

- Close the pet resource integrity contract open question on whether non-PetDex user-imported pets should become a separate `user_managed` trust tier.

Files/Areas:

- `app/src/main/java/com/nbg/android/NbgPetResourceIntegrity.kt`
- `app/src/main/java/com/nbg/android/NbgPetStore.kt`
- `app/src/main/java/com/nbg/android/NbgDiagnosticsExport.kt`
- `app/src/test/java/com/nbg/android/NbgPetResourceIntegrityTest.kt`
- `app/src/test/java/com/nbg/android/NbgDiagnosticsExportTest.kt`
- `app/src/test/java/com/nbg/android/AndroidManifestBehaviorTest.kt`
- `docs/contracts/pet-resource-integrity-contract.md`
- `docs/issues/2026-07-04-001-m1-p0-issue-queue.md`

Acceptance:

- Pet resource source kinds include `user_managed`.
- Pet trust tiers include `user_managed`.
- Local, side-loaded, custom, imported, manual, and user-managed Pet source labels classify as `user_managed`, remain review-required, and do not enter the trusted bundled default set.
- Explicit PetDex sources (`petdex`, `petdex_https`) remain `unverified_petdex` and review-required.
- Built-in trust requires `builtIn=true` or trusted bundled provenance; a raw `built-in` / `built_in` label alone is blocked.
- Blank or unrecognized persisted source strings remain `blocked`, not legacy PetDex.
- Diagnostics export includes `pets.userManagedCount`.
- Diagnostics `currentSource` exports only the normalized `sourceKind` wire value, not raw Pet source strings, URLs, tokens, or local paths.
- PetDex provenance counts use the shared Pet source classifier so both `petdex` and `petdex_https` are counted consistently.
- `pet-resource-integrity-contract.md` documents `user_managed`, normalized diagnostics source export, explicit legacy PetDex compatibility, and removes the resolved open question.
- Verification remains debug-only and does not run release/R8.


- `nbgPetSummarySourceReview()` keeps explicit `petdex` and `petdex_https` as `unverified_petdex`.
- `nbgPetSummarySourceReview()` blocks raw `built-in` / `built_in` labels unless `builtIn=true` or sibling bundled provenance proves trusted source kind, trusted tier, `requiresReview=false`, and matching slug.
- `NbgPetStore.parsePet()` preserves a blank/missing persisted `source` instead of defaulting it to `petdex`.
- `NbgPetStore` now persists and restores the `builtIn` flag for seeded bundled Pet summaries.
- `NbgDiagnosticsExport.kt` emits `pets.userManagedCount`.
- `NbgDiagnosticsExport.kt` emits normalized `currentSource = nbgPetSummarySourceReview(currentPet).sourceKind.wireName`, not the raw source label.
- `NbgDiagnosticsExport.kt` uses the shared source classifier for PetDex provenance counts, covering both `petdex` and `petdex_https`.

## Issue 63: Hanako Event Machine Readable Schema Manifest

Status: code/contract/test/review complete for Android-side machine-readable Hanako event schema metadata. The Hanako event contract now has a v1 schema manifest and a Draft 2020-12 JSON Schema projection covering every supported event type without embedding raw event frames, payload examples, session paths, tool args/results, terminal output, file content/diffs, Memory content, or conversation text. Verification remains debug-only and does not run release/R8.

Owner Agent: Agent Platform Agent + QA/Release Agent

Review Agent: QA/Release + Security Review Agent

Security Review: required because event schema metadata names sensitive event fields and must not encode raw payload examples or diagnostics-leaking content

Goal:

- Close the Android Hanako event contract open question on whether Hanako should publish a machine-readable JSON schema for every event type, using an Android-side v1 schema manifest and Draft JSON Schema projection.

Files/Areas:

- `app/src/main/java/com/nbg/android/NbgHanakoEventContract.kt`
- `app/src/test/java/com/nbg/android/NbgHanakoEventContractTest.kt`
- `app/src/test/java/com/nbg/android/AndroidManifestBehaviorTest.kt`
- `docs/contracts/android-hanako-event-contract.md`
- `docs/issues/2026-07-04-001-m1-p0-issue-queue.md`

Acceptance:

- `NBG_HANAKO_EVENT_SCHEMA_VERSION` is defined alongside the existing event contract version.
- Every type in `NBG_HANAKO_SUPPORTED_EVENT_TYPES` has exactly one `NbgHanakoEventSchemaEntry`.
- Each schema entry records event type, `NbgHanakoEventKind`, required fields, optional fields, sensitive fields, terminal-event flag, and notes.
- Schema validation detects duplicate, missing, unknown, missing-`type`, or kind-mismatched entries.
- `nbgHanakoEventSchemaJson()` exports the internal schema manifest as machine-readable JSON.
- `nbgHanakoEventJsonSchema()` exports a Draft 2020-12 JSON Schema projection with a top-level `type` enum and one `oneOf` variant per supported event type.
- JSON/schema exports contain metadata only and do not embed raw WebSocket frames, raw event payload examples, session path values, tool args/results, terminal output, file content/diffs, Memory content, or conversation text.
- Required fields stay compatibility-safe: every event requires `type`, `stream_resume` additionally requires `sessionPath`, and `confirmation_resolved` additionally requires `confirmId` and `action`.
- `android-hanako-event-contract.md` documents the manifest shape, JSON Schema projection, schema rules, and removes the resolved open question.
- Verification remains debug-only and does not run release/R8.


## Issue 64: Confirmation Resolution Local Audit Signature

Status: code/contract/test/review complete for local confirmation resolution audit entries. Confirmation resolution now carries a local integrity audit entry with hashed confirmation metadata and a canonical SHA-256 signature, and compact confirmation timeline cards show a short audit prefix without storing raw confirmation ids, bodies, target paths, tool args/results, terminal output, file diffs, Memory content, or conversation text. Verification remains debug-only and does not run release/R8.

Owner Agent: Agent Platform Agent + Security Agent

Review Agent: Security/QA Review Agent

Security Review: required because confirmation resolution is the user approval boundary for risky file, terminal, Skill, Memory, MCP, and external resource operations

Goal:

- Close the Android Hanako event contract open question on whether confirmation resolution should carry a signed local audit entry for public beta support.

Files/Areas:

- `app/src/main/java/com/nbg/android/NbgHanakoEventContract.kt`
- `app/src/main/java/com/nbg/android/HanakoBridge.kt`
- `app/src/main/java/com/nbg/android/NbgAgentUi.kt`
- `app/src/main/java/com/nbg/android/NbgToolVisualizationEventContract.kt`
- `app/src/test/java/com/nbg/android/NbgHanakoEventContractTest.kt`
- `app/src/test/java/com/nbg/android/NbgToolVisualizationEventContractTest.kt`
- `app/src/test/java/com/nbg/android/AndroidManifestBehaviorTest.kt`
- `docs/contracts/android-hanako-event-contract.md`
- `docs/issues/2026-07-04-001-m1-p0-issue-queue.md`

Acceptance:

- `NBG_CONFIRMATION_RESOLUTION_AUDIT_SCHEMA_VERSION` defines the local audit schema version.
- `NbgConfirmationResolutionAuditEntry` records schema version, hashed confirm id, normalized action, result, risk tier, hashed subject, hashed target, timestamp, and signature.
- Audit signatures are local integrity signatures over canonical v1 metadata and are documented as not remote attestation.
- Audit JSON does not contain raw confirm id, confirmation body, raw target path, command/tool args, terminal output, file content/diffs, Memory content, conversation text, or raw event payload.
- Invalid non-null audit entries are rejected and rebuilt locally before pending confirmation state is cleared.
- Confirmation timeline card keys use hashed `confirmIdHash` prefixes, not raw confirmation ids.
- Timeline cards may display only a short audit signature prefix for support correlation.
- Duplicate confirmation resolution merge preserves richer audit context and audit prefix.
- `android-hanako-event-contract.md` documents local audit entry semantics and now lists `None for v1` open questions.
- Verification remains debug-only and does not run release/R8.

Verification:

```bash
./gradlew --no-daemon :app:testDebugUnitTest --tests com.nbg.android.NbgHanakoEventContractTest --tests com.nbg.android.NbgToolVisualizationEventContractTest --tests com.nbg.android.AndroidManifestBehaviorTest.androidHanakoEventContractIsDocumentedAndWired --tests com.nbg.android.AndroidManifestBehaviorTest.toolVisualizationEventContractIsDocumentedAndWired
./gradlew --no-daemon :app:testDebugUnitTest :terminal-core:testDebugUnitTest :android-mcp-server:testDebugUnitTest :app:assembleDebug :android-mcp-server:assembleDebug
```

Evidence:

- `NbgHanakoEventContract.kt` now defines `NBG_CONFIRMATION_RESOLUTION_AUDIT_SCHEMA_VERSION = "nbg-confirmation-resolution-audit-v1"`.
- `NbgConfirmationResolutionAuditEntry` is the public value object carried by `HanakoChatEvent.ConfirmationResolved`.
- `nbgBuildConfirmationResolutionAuditEntry()` normalizes action/result, hashes confirm id, subject, and target fields, clamps timestamp to non-negative, and signs canonical metadata with SHA-256.
- `nbgVerifyConfirmationResolutionAuditEntry()` verifies schema version and detects field tampering by recomputing the canonical signature.
- `toConfirmationAuditJson()` exports audit metadata only.
- `nbgConfirmationAuditTimelineLabel()` exposes only a short signature prefix for timeline support correlation.
- `NbgAgentUi` accepts only valid `event.auditEntry`; invalid or missing entries are replaced by a local audit entry before `confirmationState.resolveConfirmation()` clears pending context.
- `nbgConfirmationResolutionToolStatus()` uses hashed `confirmIdHash.take(32)` in the `HanakoToolStatus.key` and only includes the short audit prefix in detail.
- `HanakoChatEvent.ConfirmationResolved` now has optional `auditEntry`.
- `NbgHanakoEventContractTest.confirmationResolutionAuditEntrySignsHashedLocalMetadataOnly` verifies local signature fields, tamper detection, short timeline label, and absence of raw confirm id/body/path in audit JSON.
- `NbgToolVisualizationEventContractTest.confirmationResolutionProducesCompactTimelineStatus` verifies audit prefix display, hashed key behavior, and absence of raw confirm id or full signature in visible detail.
- `NbgToolVisualizationEventContractTest.duplicateConfirmationResolutionMergePreservesAuditContext` verifies duplicate/fallback merge preserves the audit prefix and richer confirmation context.
- `AndroidManifestBehaviorTest.androidHanakoEventContractIsDocumentedAndWired` locks audit constants, builder/verify helpers, event payload field, UI fallback behavior, contract text, and open-question removal.
- `AndroidManifestBehaviorTest.toolVisualizationEventContractIsDocumentedAndWired` locks hashed key and audit timeline label use in the tool visualization contract.
- `tool-visualization-event-contract.md` now documents `confirmation:<confirmIdHashPrefix>` keys and forbids keeping raw confirmation ids in compact confirmation timeline keys.
- `android-hanako-event-contract.md` documents local audit entry fields, local integrity signature semantics, raw-data prohibitions, diagnostics exclusion, test oracle, and `None for v1` open questions.
- Initial read-only Security/QA Review Agent pass found one Medium issue and two Low issues: invalid non-null audit entries were not rebuilt, timeline keys retained raw confirm ids, and duplicate merge tests did not cover audit prefix preservation. Follow-up fixed invalid audit fallback, switched keys to hashed confirm id prefixes, and added audit-bearing merge coverage.

## Issue 65: Skill Trusted External Promotion Policy Gate

Status: code/contract/test/review complete for the trusted external Skill promotion policy gate. Public-beta v1 now explicitly has no trusted-external Skill tier and no external Skill promotion path; future promotion requires both signed bundle evidence and pinned SHA-256 allowlist evidence, while v1 always keeps external/user/path/URL Skills review-required and out of trusted defaults. Verification remains debug-only and does not run release/R8.

Owner Agent: Security Agent + Agent Platform Agent

Review Agent: Security/QA Review Agent

Security Review: required because external Skill promotion controls whether downloaded, user-managed, or Agent-written code can become a trusted default capability

Goal:

- Close the Skill source integrity contract open question on whether trusted external Skills should use signed bundles, pinned SHA-256 allowlists, or both.

Files/Areas:

- `app/src/main/java/com/nbg/android/NbgSkillSourceIntegrity.kt`
- `app/src/test/java/com/nbg/android/NbgSkillSourceIntegrityTest.kt`
- `app/src/test/java/com/nbg/android/AndroidManifestBehaviorTest.kt`
- `docs/contracts/skill-source-integrity-contract.md`
- `docs/issues/2026-07-04-001-m1-p0-issue-queue.md`

Acceptance:

- `NBG_SKILL_TRUSTED_EXTERNAL_PROMOTION_POLICY` defines the v1 promotion policy.
- `NBG_SKILL_TRUSTED_EXTERNAL_REQUIRED_EVIDENCE` lists both `signed_bundle` and `pinned_sha256_allowlist`.
- `NbgExternalSkillPromotionReview` records policy version, eligibility, trust tier, review requirement, required evidence, present evidence, and reason.
- `nbgReviewExternalSkillTrustedPromotion()` always returns `eligibleForTrustedDefault=false` in public-beta v1.
- Even when both future evidence labels are present, v1 does not promote external/user/path/URL Skills into a trusted default tier.
- Contract text states future promotion requires both signed bundle evidence and pinned SHA-256 allowlist evidence, not either alone.

## Issue 66: PetDex Manifest Provenance Cache

Status: code/contract/test/debug verification complete for separate PetDex manifest provenance caching. Android now records manifest provenance under app-private pet storage before exposing a refreshed manifest to UI, keeps that record separate from installed pet provenance, and exports only a redacted diagnostics summary. Verification remains debug-only and does not run release/R8.

Owner Agent: Security Agent + UX/UI Agent

Review Agent: Security/QA Review Agent requested; independent Codex CLI review was attempted but could not complete in this non-git workspace/read-only review combination. Lead self-review and debug verification completed; rerun independent review when the sub-agent/review transport is available.

Security Review: required because PetDex manifest data crosses the network boundary and diagnostics may leave the app by user share.

Goal:

- Close the Pet resource integrity contract open question on whether Android should cache manifest provenance separately from installed resource provenance.

Files/Areas:

- `app/src/main/java/com/nbg/android/NbgPetResourceIntegrity.kt`
- `app/src/main/java/com/nbg/android/NbgPetDexClient.kt`
- `app/src/main/java/com/nbg/android/NbgAgentUi.kt`
- `app/src/main/java/com/nbg/android/NbgDiagnosticsExport.kt`
- `app/src/test/java/com/nbg/android/NbgPetResourceIntegrityTest.kt`
- `app/src/test/java/com/nbg/android/NbgDiagnosticsExportTest.kt`
- `app/src/test/java/com/nbg/android/AndroidManifestBehaviorTest.kt`
- `docs/contracts/pet-resource-integrity-contract.md`
- `docs/issues/2026-07-04-001-m1-p0-issue-queue.md`

Acceptance:

- `NBG_PETDEX_MANIFEST_PROVENANCE_FILE` defines the manifest provenance file name.
- `NbgPetDexManifestProvenance` records schema, source kind, trust tier, review requirement, source host, redacted source, size, sha256, max bytes, declared total, parsed pet count, and fetch timestamp.
- `nbgWritePetDexManifestProvenance()` writes only metadata and hashes, not the raw manifest body or pet list.
- `nbgReadPetDexManifestProvenance()` accepts only v1 `petdex_https` + `unverified_petdex` + `requiresReview=true` manifest records within size/hash policy.
- `NbgPetDexClient.fetchManifest()` writes manifest provenance after final URL review and manifest parsing, before returning the manifest to UI.
- `NbgAgentUi` constructs `NbgPetDexClient` with app-private `filesDir/nbg-pets` provenance storage.
- Diagnostics export includes manifest provenance presence, source kind, trust tier, review flag, counts, timestamp, and sha256 prefix only.
- Diagnostics export does not include raw PetDex URL, query string, userinfo, redacted URL, source host, manifest body, pet list, downloaded contents, or local path.
- The Pet resource integrity contract removes the resolved manifest provenance cache open question.
- Verification remains debug-only and does not run release/R8.

Verification:

```bash
./gradlew --no-daemon :app:testDebugUnitTest --tests com.nbg.android.NbgPetResourceIntegrityTest --tests com.nbg.android.NbgDiagnosticsExportTest --tests com.nbg.android.AndroidManifestBehaviorTest.petResourceIntegrityContractIsDocumentedAndWired --tests com.nbg.android.AndroidManifestBehaviorTest.androidExposesPetdexPetManagementControls --tests com.nbg.android.AndroidManifestBehaviorTest.petdexManifestParserSupportsSearchAndCharacterFiltering

```

Evidence:

- `NbgPetResourceIntegrity.kt` defines `NBG_PETDEX_MANIFEST_PROVENANCE_FILE = ".nbg-petdex-manifest-provenance.json"`.
- `NbgPetDexManifestProvenance` is the manifest-level value object and stays separate from per-installed-pet provenance.
- `nbgPetDexManifestProvenanceDir(filesDir)` resolves app-private `filesDir/nbg-pets`.
- `nbgWritePetDexManifestProvenance()` writes v1 metadata with `petdex_https`, `unverified_petdex`, `requiresReview=true`, redacted source, original response byte size, original response byte sha256, declared total, parsed pet count, and timestamp.
- `nbgReadPetDexManifestProvenance()` rejects wrong schema, wrong source kind, wrong trust tier, missing review requirement, unsafe host, non-redacted location, invalid sha256, oversize records, and invalid counts/timestamps.
- `NbgPetDexClient.fetchManifest()` hashes original manifest response bytes and writes provenance after final redirect URL review.
- `NbgAgentUi` passes `nbgPetDexManifestProvenanceDir(context.filesDir)` to `NbgPetDexClient`.
- `NbgDiagnosticsExport` reads manifest provenance from app-private storage and exports only `manifestProvenancePresent`, source kind, trust tier, review flag, declared total, parsed pet count, fetch timestamp, and sha256 prefix.

## Issue 71: Ubuntu Rootfs Runtime Checksum Gate

Status: code/runbook/test/debug verification complete for the Ubuntu rootfs runtime checksum gate. Public-beta v1 now verifies the landed Ubuntu rootfs archive SHA-256 immediately before extraction, binds the copied asset version marker to the checksum, and deletes a mismatched archive so the next terminal environment prepare can recopy the packaged asset. Verification remains debug-only and does not run release/R8.

Owner Agent: Terminal/Runtime Agent + Security Agent

Review Agent: Security/QA Review Agent requested for the broader Issue 67-72 evidence refresh; lead self-review and debug verification completed.

Security Review: required because the Ubuntu rootfs archive is a large runtime supply-chain asset used before the local IDE execution loop becomes available.

Goal:

- Close the gap where the source Ubuntu rootfs asset had checksum inventory coverage but the runtime installer only checked file presence before extracting the landed archive.

Files/Areas:

- `terminal-core/src/main/java/com/nbg/android/terminal/UbuntuBootstrapScript.kt`
- `terminal-core/src/main/java/com/nbg/android/terminal/TerminalEnvironment.kt`
- `terminal-core/src/test/java/com/nbg/android/terminal/NbgCodeRuntimeTest.kt`
- `terminal-core/src/test/java/com/nbg/android/terminal/UbuntuBootstrapScriptTest.kt`
- `terminal-core/src/test/java/com/nbg/android/terminal/TerminalRuntimeAssetInventoryTest.kt`
- `app/src/test/java/com/nbg/android/AndroidManifestBehaviorTest.kt`
- `docs/runbooks/terminal-build-reliability-baseline.md`
- `docs/runbooks/terminal-runtime-asset-checksum-inventory.md`
- `docs/issues/2026-07-04-001-m1-p0-issue-queue.md`

Acceptance:

- `UbuntuBootstrapScript.UBUNTU_ARCHIVE_SHA256` matches the terminal runtime asset inventory SHA-256 for `ubuntu-noble-aarch64-pd-v4.18.0.tar.xz`.
- `UbuntuBootstrapScript.UBUNTU_ARCHIVE_VERSION` includes the archive name and SHA-256 so copied asset refresh is checksum-aware.
- `TerminalEnvironment.copyVersionedAsset()` uses `UbuntuBootstrapScript.UBUNTU_ARCHIVE_VERSION` for the Ubuntu archive marker.
- The rendered bootstrap script exports `UBUNTU_ARCHIVE_SHA256`.
- `install_ubuntu()` computes the landed archive SHA-256 before `tar xf`.
- Missing checksum output and checksum mismatch fail before extraction.
- Checksum mismatch removes the landed archive so a later prepare can recopy the packaged asset.
- The terminal reliability runbook states the shell verifies SHA-256 before rootfs extraction.
- The runtime asset inventory states the Ubuntu SHA-256 constant must stay synchronized with the inventory.
- Verification remains debug-only and does not run release/R8.

Verification:

```bash
./gradlew --no-daemon :terminal-core:testDebugUnitTest --tests com.nbg.android.terminal.UbuntuBootstrapScriptTest --tests com.nbg.android.terminal.NbgCodeRuntimeTest --tests com.nbg.android.terminal.TerminalRuntimeAssetInventoryTest :app:testDebugUnitTest --tests com.nbg.android.AndroidManifestBehaviorTest.terminalEnvironmentRefreshesVersionedRuntimeAssets
./gradlew --no-daemon :app:testDebugUnitTest :terminal-core:testDebugUnitTest :android-mcp-server:testDebugUnitTest :app:assembleDebug :android-mcp-server:assembleDebug
```

Evidence:

- `UbuntuBootstrapScript.kt` defines `UBUNTU_ARCHIVE_SHA256 = "91acaa786b8e2fbba56a9fd0f8a1188cee482b5c7baeed707b29ddaa9a294daa"`.
- `UbuntuBootstrapScript.kt` defines `UBUNTU_ARCHIVE_VERSION = UBUNTU_ARCHIVE + "-" + UBUNTU_ARCHIVE_SHA256`.
- `TerminalEnvironment.kt` copies the Ubuntu archive with `version = UbuntuBootstrapScript.UBUNTU_ARCHIVE_VERSION`.
- The rendered bootstrap script exports `UBUNTU_ARCHIVE_SHA256`, computes `actual_ubuntu_sha` with busybox `sha256sum`, emits bounded mismatch diagnostics, removes the mismatched archive, and only then runs `tar xf "$ubuntu_archive_path"`.
- `NbgCodeRuntimeTest.runtimeAssetNamesMatchAndroidPackagedRuntime` locks the Ubuntu archive name, SHA-256, and version marker.
- `UbuntuBootstrapScriptTest.renderedScriptVerifiesUbuntuArchiveChecksumBeforeExtraction` verifies checksum export, checksum calculation, failure messages, mismatch cleanup, and that checksum calculation appears before extraction.
- `TerminalRuntimeAssetInventoryTest` verifies the source Ubuntu rootfs asset size `64133552` and SHA-256 `91acaa786b8e2fbba56a9fd0f8a1188cee482b5c7baeed707b29ddaa9a294daa`.
- `AndroidManifestBehaviorTest.terminalEnvironmentRefreshesVersionedRuntimeAssets` locks the checksum-aware asset marker, bootstrap checksum gate, and runbook wording from the app source oracle.
- `terminal-build-reliability-baseline.md` now states the shell verifies SHA-256 before rootfs extraction.
- `terminal-runtime-asset-checksum-inventory.md` now states `UbuntuBootstrapScript.UBUNTU_ARCHIVE_SHA256` must remain synchronized with the inventory and the runtime install gate must reject mismatched archives before extraction.
- Targeted verification passes: `./gradlew --no-daemon :terminal-core:testDebugUnitTest --tests com.nbg.android.terminal.UbuntuBootstrapScriptTest --tests com.nbg.android.terminal.NbgCodeRuntimeTest --tests com.nbg.android.terminal.TerminalRuntimeAssetInventoryTest :app:testDebugUnitTest --tests com.nbg.android.AndroidManifestBehaviorTest.terminalEnvironmentRefreshesVersionedRuntimeAssets`.

## Issue 72: Independent Review Evidence Refresh And MCP Clipboard Boundary Fix

Status: independent Security/QA Review Agent pass completed for Issues 67-70, one MCP clipboard boundary finding fixed in code/contract/test, and current test-result XML evidence refreshed for Issues 67-71. The evidence refresh remains debug-only and does not run release/R8.

Owner Agent: Security Agent + QA/Release Agent + Android MCP Agent

Review Agent: independent Security/QA Review Agent completed read-only inspection and reported two Medium findings: MCP contract forbade clipboard reads while the MCP token TTL clear path read clipboard contents, and Issue 67-70 historical verification claims were not supported by current XML artifacts at review time.

Security Review: required because the fix changes local bearer-token clipboard handling and verifies public-beta gates for resource trust, Memory policy, and MCP boundaries.

Goal:

- Close the newly available independent review loop for Issues 67-70 and remove the MCP clipboard read privacy mismatch.
- Refresh current test artifacts so Issue 67-70 evidence is backed by present XML results, not only historical command logs.

Files/Areas:

- `android-mcp-server/src/main/java/com/nbg/android/mcpserver/MainActivity.kt`
- `android-mcp-server/src/test/java/com/nbg/android/mcpserver/McpServerBoundaryTest.kt`
- `docs/contracts/android-mcp-server-contract.md`
- `docs/contracts/local-service-boundary-contract.md`
- `docs/issues/2026-07-04-001-m1-p0-issue-queue.md`

Acceptance:

- MCP token copy still writes the bearer token only on user tap and marks the clip sensitive on Android 13+.
- The TTL clear path must not call `clipboard.primaryClip` or `coerceToText`.
- The TTL clear path clears the token only when no primary-clip change has been observed after the token copy.
- Clipboard listener cleanup runs after TTL handling and Activity destruction.
- The Android MCP contract states token-copy UI may write the token and observe primary-clip change notifications, but must not read clipboard contents.
- The local service boundary contract states token TTL clearing must not read clipboard contents.
- `McpServerBoundaryTest` source oracle forbids `clipboard.primaryClip` and `coerceToText` in `MainActivity`.
- Current XML evidence exists for `NbgPetResourceIntegrityTest`, `NbgMemoryContextPolicyTest`, `AndroidManifestBehaviorTest`, `McpServerBoundaryTest`, and Issue 71 terminal tests with 0 failures/errors.
- Verification remains debug-only and does not run release/R8.

## Issue 73: Release Gate Skip Flag Auditability And Public Beta Guard

Status: script/doc/test/debug verification complete for release gate skip flag auditability. Local acceleration flags remain available for iteration, but the release gate summary now marks skipped lint/tests as not public-beta eligible, and `NBG_RELEASE_PUBLIC_BETA_CANDIDATE=1` fails fast if lint or tests are skipped. Verification includes a skipped local release-gate run and public-beta skip guard check; full public-beta release gate without skip flags was not rerun in this issue.

Owner Agent: QA/Release Agent + Security Agent

Review Agent: Lead self-review completed; rerun independent QA/Release review before a real public-beta candidate artifact is approved.

Security Review: required because release-gate output can be reused as public-beta evidence and must not allow skipped lint/tests to masquerade as approval.

Goal:

- Make `NBG_RELEASE_SKIP_LINT` and `NBG_RELEASE_SKIP_TESTS` machine-visible in release-gate output and prevent their use when explicitly running a public-beta candidate gate.

Files/Areas:

- `scripts/nbg_release_gate.sh`
- `docs/release/2026-07-04-001-release-build-hygiene-and-supply-chain.md`
- `app/src/test/java/com/nbg/android/AndroidManifestBehaviorTest.kt`
- `docs/issues/2026-07-04-001-m1-p0-issue-queue.md`

Acceptance:

- `scripts/nbg_release_gate.sh` reads `NBG_RELEASE_SKIP_LINT`, `NBG_RELEASE_SKIP_TESTS`, and `NBG_RELEASE_PUBLIC_BETA_CANDIDATE` into explicit variables.
- When `NBG_RELEASE_PUBLIC_BETA_CANDIDATE=1` and either skip flag is set, the script exits with status `2` before running Gradle.
- The failure message names both forbidden skip flags.
- `summary.txt` records `public_beta_candidate`, `lint_skipped`, `tests_skipped`, `public_beta_eligible`, and `skip_reason`.
- A skipped local iteration run writes `public_beta_eligible=false` and `skip_reason=local_iteration_only`.
- A non-skipped run writes `public_beta_eligible=true` and `skip_reason=none`.
- Release hygiene docs describe public-beta candidate mode and the required summary fields.
- `AndroidManifestBehaviorTest.releaseBuildHygieneAndSupplyChainGateIsDocumented` locks the script guard, summary fields, and docs.
- Verification remains debug-first; a skipped local release gate may assemble release artifacts but must not be treated as public-beta approval.
